### Task 7: Routes, the plugin contract and configuration checks

`route()` with its inferred types (spec §4), the server half of the plugin contract (§8, no plugins), and `readConfig`,
which `createCloudrop` calls in Task 8 to fail fast on every configuration mistake.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/cloudrop/src/server/route.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/plugins.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/config.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cloudrop/src/storage/validate.ts`
- Test: `packages/cloudrop/src/server/plugins.test.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/config.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `configError`, `fromEnv`, `formatBytes` (now exported from `validate.ts`), `isAcceptEntry`, `parseSize`,
  `Size`, `importTokenKeys`, `TokenKeys`, `StandardSchema`, `SchemaInput`, `SchemaOutput`, `Jsonified`,
  `UploadedFile`, `StorageDriver`.
- Produces (`route.ts`):
  - `type Awaitable<T>`; `interface Unauthorized`; `unauthorized(message?: string): Unauthorized`;
    `isUnauthorized(value: unknown): value is Unauthorized`; `allowAnyone(): object`.
  - `interface AuthorizeContext<TMetadata>`; `type KeyContext<TAuth, TMetadata>`; `type CompleteContext<TAuth,
    TMetadata>`.
  - `interface RouteOptions<TAuth extends object, TSchema extends StandardSchema | undefined, TResult>`;
    `interface RouteDefinition` (erased); `interface Route<TMetadataInput = unknown, TResult = unknown> { readonly
    definition: RouteDefinition; readonly "~types"?: … }`.
  - `route<TAuth extends object, TSchema extends StandardSchema | undefined = undefined, TResult = null>(options:
    RouteOptions<TAuth, TSchema, TResult>): Route<SchemaInput<TSchema>, Jsonified<Awaited<TResult>>>`.
- Produces (`plugins.ts`): `PluginFile`, `BeforeUploadContext`, `AfterVerifyContext`, `AbortContext`, `ServerHooks`,
  `CloudropPlugin`, `Logger`; `runOnAbort(plugins, contexts, logger): Promise<void>`; `runBeforeUpload(plugins,
  context, logger): Promise<CloudropError | undefined>`; `runAfterVerify(plugins, context): Promise<CloudropError |
  undefined>`.
- Produces (`config.ts`): `interface CloudropOptions<TRoutes>`; `interface RouteConfig { name; definition; accept:
  readonly string[]; maxSize: number; maxFiles: number; visibility }`; `interface ServerConfig { storage; routes:
  ReadonlyMap<string, RouteConfig>; tokenKeys: Promise<TokenKeys>; basePath; trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>;
  multipartThreshold: number | undefined; plugins; logger }`; `ROUTE_NAME: RegExp`; `readConfig(options):
  ServerConfig`; `interface EndpointInput { request: Request; body: unknown; config: ServerConfig }`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`readConfig` is tested directly: it is the whole of `createCloudrop`'s checking. Every row calls it the way plain
JavaScript may, so the checks for a missing or wrongly typed option are exercised.

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/plugins.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { CloudropError } from "../errors.ts";
import {
  type BeforeUploadContext,
  type CloudropPlugin,
  runAfterVerify,
  runBeforeUpload,
  runOnAbort,
} from "./plugins.ts";

/** Records what the runners log. */
function recordingLogger() {
  const errors: { message: string; error: unknown }[] = [];
  return { errors, error: (message: string, error?: unknown) => void errors.push({ message, error }), warn: () => {} };
}

const file = { id: "f1", key: "k/f1.txt", name: "a.txt", size: 1, type: "text/plain" };
const context: BeforeUploadContext = {
  request: new Request("https://app.example.com/api/cloudrop/upload"),
  route: "files",
  files: [file],
  metadata: undefined,
  auth: {},
};

function releasing(id: string, events: string[]): CloudropPlugin {
  return {
    id,
    server: {
      beforeUpload: () => void events.push(`${id} reserved`),
      onAbort: ({ reason }) => void events.push(`${id} released (${reason})`),
    },
  };
}

describe("runBeforeUpload", () => {
  it("releases what earlier plugins reserved when a later one throws, then lets the error through", async () => {
    const events: string[] = [];
    const broken = new Error("limiter unavailable");
    const throwing: CloudropPlugin = {
      id: "limits",
      server: {
        beforeUpload: () => {
          throw broken;
        },
        onAbort: () => void events.push("limits released"),
      },
    };
    const run = runBeforeUpload([releasing("first", events), throwing], context, recordingLogger());
    await expect(run).rejects.toBe(broken);
    // Fail-closed: the upload stops, and the plugin that failed is not asked to release what it never reserved.
    expect(events).toEqual(["first reserved", "first released (failed)"]);
  });

  it("refuses a hook that returns something other than a CloudropError", async () => {
    const sloppy = { id: "sloppy", server: { beforeUpload: () => "no" } } as unknown as CloudropPlugin;
    await expect(runBeforeUpload([sloppy], context, recordingLogger())).rejects.toThrow(
      'Plugin "sloppy": beforeUpload must return a CloudropError or nothing.',
    );
  });
});

describe("runOnAbort", () => {
  it("logs a failing onAbort and still runs the plugins after it", async () => {
    const events: string[] = [];
    const failure = new Error("quota store down");
    const failing: CloudropPlugin = {
      id: "failing",
      server: {
        onAbort: () => {
          throw failure;
        },
      },
    };
    const logger = recordingLogger();
    await runOnAbort(
      [failing, releasing("after", events)],
      [{ route: "files", file, metadata: undefined, auth: {}, reason: "aborted" }],
      logger,
    );
    expect({ events, errors: logger.errors }).toEqual({
      events: ["after released (aborted)"],
      errors: [{ message: 'cloudrop: plugin "failing" failed in onAbort for file f1.', error: failure }],
    });
  });
});

describe("runAfterVerify", () => {
  it("stops at the first refusal", async () => {
    const refusal = new CloudropError("VERIFICATION_FAILED", "Refused by the first plugin.");
    const seen: string[] = [];
    const plugins: CloudropPlugin[] = [
      { id: "first", server: { afterVerify: () => refusal } },
      { id: "second", server: { afterVerify: () => void seen.push("second") } },
    ];
    const verified = {
      request: context.request,
      route: "files",
      file: { ...file, url: null },
      metadata: undefined,
      auth: {},
      storage: {} as never,
    };
    expect([await runAfterVerify(plugins, verified), seen]).toEqual([refusal, []]);
  });
});
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/config.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { r2 } from "../storage/presets.ts";
import { type CloudropOptions, readConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { allowAnyone, type Route, route } from "./route.ts";
import { importTokenKey, signToken, verifyToken } from "./token.ts";

const storage = r2({
  bucket: "uploads",
  accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
  accessKeyId: "k",
  secretAccessKey: "s",
});
const publicStorage = r2({
  bucket: "uploads",
  accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
  accessKeyId: "k",
  secretAccessKey: "s",
  publicUrl: "https://cdn.example.com",
});
const files = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1MB", authorize: allowAnyone });

const SECRET = "test-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456789";

afterEach(() => {
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

/** readConfig, as createCloudrop calls it, with sound defaults and one override, called the way plain JavaScript may call it. */
function create(overrides: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return () =>
    readConfig({ storage, secret: SECRET, routes: { files }, ...overrides } as CloudropOptions<Record<string, Route>>);
}

function invalidRoute(options: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return create({
    routes: { avatar: { definition: { accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1MB", authorize: allowAnyone, ...options } } },
  });
}

describe("readConfig errors", () => {
  it.for<{ name: string; make: () => unknown; message: string }>([
    {
      name: "no options",
      make: () => (readConfig as (options?: unknown) => unknown)(),
      message: "Pass storage to createCloudrop: r2({ bucket }) or s3({ ... }).",
    },
    {
      name: "no routes",
      make: create({ routes: {} }),
      message: "Pass at least one route: routes: { avatar: route({ ... }) }.",
    },
    {
      name: "a route name with a slash",
      make: create({ routes: { "a/b": files } }),
      message: "Route names may use letters, digits, - and _ (at most 64), such as avatar or post-image.",
    },
    {
      name: "a route not made by route()",
      make: create({ routes: { avatar: { accept: "*/*" } } }),
      message: 'Route "avatar" is not a route. Wrap its options in route({ ... }).',
    },
    {
      name: "a route without authorize",
      make: invalidRoute({ authorize: undefined }),
      message: 'Route "avatar" needs authorize. Use authorize: allowAnyone for a route anyone may upload to.',
    },
    {
      name: "an empty accept list",
      make: invalidRoute({ accept: [] }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": accept takes MIME types in lower case, such as "image/png", "image/*" or "*/*".',
    },
    {
      name: "an extension in accept",
      make: invalidRoute({ accept: ".png" }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": accept takes MIME types in lower case, such as "image/png", "image/*" or "*/*".',
    },
    {
      name: "a maxSize of 0",
      make: invalidRoute({ maxSize: 0 }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": maxSize must be between 1 byte and 5115 GiB, such as 5242880 or "5MB".',
    },
    {
      name: "a maxSize past R2's object limit",
      make: invalidRoute({ maxSize: "5TB" }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": maxSize must be between 1 byte and 5115 GiB, such as 5242880 or "5MB".',
    },
    {
      name: "a maxSize with a space",
      make: invalidRoute({ maxSize: "5 MB" }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": maxSize must be between 1 byte and 5115 GiB, such as 5242880 or "5MB".',
    },
    {
      name: "maxFiles of 101",
      make: invalidRoute({ maxFiles: 101 }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": maxFiles must be a whole number from 1 to 100.',
    },
    {
      name: "a metadata value that is no schema",
      make: invalidRoute({ metadata: { albumId: "string" } }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": metadata must be a Standard Schema, such as a zod, valibot or arktype schema.',
    },
    {
      name: "a prefix that is a string",
      make: invalidRoute({ prefix: "avatars" }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": prefix must be a function.',
    },
    {
      name: "both prefix and key",
      make: invalidRoute({ prefix: () => "a", key: () => "b" }),
      message: 'Route "avatar": pass prefix or key, not both. key builds the whole key; prefix only its folder.',
    },
    {
      name: "a public route without publicUrl",
      make: invalidRoute({ visibility: "public" }),
      message:
        'Route "avatar" is public, but the storage has no publicUrl. Pass publicUrl to the storage preset, or make the route private.',
    },
    {
      name: "a basePath with a trailing slash",
      make: create({ basePath: "/api/cloudrop/" }),
      message: 'basePath must be a path such as "/api/cloudrop": a leading slash, no trailing slash, no query.',
    },
    // "*" would hand every site a credentialed CORS answer.
    {
      name: 'a trusted origin of "*"',
      make: create({ trustedOrigins: ["*"] }),
      message:
        'trustedOrigins must list exact origins such as "https://app.example.com": scheme and host, no path or trailing slash, and never "*".',
    },
    {
      name: "a trusted origin with a trailing slash",
      make: create({ trustedOrigins: ["https://app.example.com/"] }),
      message:
        'trustedOrigins must list exact origins such as "https://app.example.com": scheme and host, no path or trailing slash, and never "*".',
    },
    {
      name: "a multipartThreshold in words",
      make: create({ multipartThreshold: "big" }),
      message: 'multipartThreshold must be a whole number of bytes or a size such as "64MB".',
    },
    {
      name: "two plugins with one id",
      make: create({ plugins: [{ id: "a" }, { id: "a" }] }),
      message: "Every plugin needs its own id. Pass each plugin once.",
    },
    {
      name: "a 31-character secret",
      make: create({ secret: "s".repeat(31) }),
      message:
        "Each upload token secret must be text of at least 32 characters. Generate one with `openssl rand -base64 32`.",
    },
    {
      name: "a short previous secret",
      make: create({ secret: [SECRET, "short"] }),
      message:
        "Each upload token secret must be text of at least 32 characters. Generate one with `openssl rand -base64 32`.",
    },
    {
      name: "an empty secret list",
      make: create({ secret: [] }),
      message:
        "Missing the upload token secret. Set CLOUDROP_SECRET or pass secret to createCloudrop(); generate one with `openssl rand -base64 32`.",
    },
  ])("throws INVALID_CONFIG for $name", ({ make, message }) => {
    expect(make).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ code: "INVALID_CONFIG", message }));
  });

  it("accepts a 32-character secret", () => {
    expect(create({ secret: "s".repeat(32) })).not.toThrow();
  });

  it("never quotes the secret in its message", () => {
    expect(create({ secret: "too-short-but-private" })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ message: expect.not.stringContaining("too-short-but-private") }),
    );
  });

  it("lets a plugin refuse a route it cannot serve", () => {
    const plugin = {
      id: "images",
      server: {
        extendRoute: ({ name }: { name: string }) => {
          throw new Error(`images() cannot serve route ${name}`);
        },
      },
    };
    expect(create({ plugins: [plugin] })).toThrow("images() cannot serve route files");
  });
});

describe("readConfig defaults", () => {
  const payload = {
    v: 1,
    route: "files",
    fileId: "f",
    key: "k",
    name: "n",
    size: 1,
    type: "text/plain",
    owner: {},
    metadata: null,
    exp: 2,
  } as const;

  it("signs tokens with the secret from CLOUDROP_SECRET", async () => {
    vi.stubEnv("CLOUDROP_SECRET", SECRET);
    const { signing } = await readConfig({ storage, routes: { files } }).tokenKeys;
    const token = await signToken(payload, signing);
    expect((await verifyToken(token, [await importTokenKey(SECRET)], 1)).data).toEqual(payload);
  });

  it("refuses to start when CLOUDROP_SECRET is empty and no secret is passed", () => {
    vi.stubEnv("CLOUDROP_SECRET", "");
    expect(() => readConfig({ storage, routes: { files } })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: "INVALID_CONFIG" }),
    );
  });

  it("makes a route public when the storage has a publicUrl, and private otherwise", () => {
    expect([
      readConfig({ storage: publicStorage, secret: SECRET, routes: { files } }).routes.get("files")?.visibility,
      readConfig({ storage, secret: SECRET, routes: { files } }).routes.get("files")?.visibility,
    ]).toEqual(["public", "private"]);
  });

  it("warns when a private route stores files on a bucket with a public URL", () => {
    const warnings: string[] = [];
    const logger = { error: () => {}, warn: (message: string) => void warnings.push(message) };
    const privateFiles = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1MB", authorize: allowAnyone, visibility: "private" });
    readConfig({ storage: publicStorage, secret: SECRET, routes: { privateFiles }, logger });
    expect(warnings).toEqual([
      'cloudrop: route "privateFiles" is private, but the storage has a publicUrl, so anyone who learns a key can read the file. Serve private files from a bucket without a public domain.',
    ]);
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- Accepting `trustedOrigins: ["*"]`: every site would get a credentialed CORS answer.
- A 31-character secret accepted, or a secret quoted in the error (it would reach logs).
- A plugin's later `beforeUpload` refusing or throwing without releasing what an earlier plugin reserved.
- A throwing `onAbort` stopping the plugins after it.
- A public route on storage without `publicUrl`: every `file.url` would be wrong.
- `CLOUDROP_SECRET` read, but a different key used to sign (the default test verifies with the env secret's own key).

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/route.ts`:

```ts
import type { Jsonified, UploadedFile } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { SchemaInput, SchemaOutput, StandardSchema } from "./schema.ts";
import type { Size } from "./size.ts";

export type Awaitable<T> = T | Promise<T>;

const DENIED: unique symbol = Symbol("cloudrop.unauthorized");

/** Returned by `authorize` to refuse an upload: the client gets UNAUTHORIZED with `message`. */
export interface Unauthorized {
  readonly [DENIED]: true;
  readonly message: string;
}

export function unauthorized(message = "Sign in to upload files here."): Unauthorized {
  return { [DENIED]: true, message };
}

export function isUnauthorized(value: unknown): value is Unauthorized {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && DENIED in value;
}

/** `authorize: allowAnyone` makes a route public. It must be written out: public uploads are never a default. */
export function allowAnyone(): object {
  return {};
}

/** Names cloudrop puts into every route context; an authorize result's own fields with these names are replaced. */
type ReservedName = "request" | "metadata" | "file";

export interface AuthorizeContext<TMetadata> {
  request: Request;
  metadata: TMetadata;
}

/** `prefix` and `key` see the live authorize result; this runs inside `/upload`. */
export type KeyContext<TAuth, TMetadata> = Omit<TAuth, ReservedName> & {
  request: Request;
  metadata: TMetadata;
  file: { id: string; name: string; size: number; type: string; ext: string };
};

/** `onComplete` runs inside `/complete`, so it sees what the token carried: the JSON form of each value. */
export type CompleteContext<TAuth, TMetadata> = Omit<Jsonified<TAuth>, ReservedName> & {
  request: Request;
  file: UploadedFile;
  metadata: Jsonified<TMetadata>;
};

export interface RouteOptions<TAuth extends object, TSchema extends StandardSchema | undefined, TResult> {
  /** MIME types such as "image/png", "image/*" or "*\/*". The file name's extension must agree with the type. */
  accept: string | readonly string[];
  /** Bytes, or "5MB" style (binary units). */
  maxSize: Size;
  /** Files per `/upload` call, 1 to 100. Default 10. */
  maxFiles?: number;
  /** Any Standard Schema (zod, valibot, arktype). Its output, as JSON, must fit in 2 KiB. */
  metadata?: TSchema;
  /**
   * Required. Return the facts later steps need (they are signed into the upload token, which the browser can read:
   * return ids, never secrets), or `unauthorized()`. `allowAnyone` makes the route public.
   */
  authorize: (context: AuthorizeContext<SchemaOutput<TSchema>>) => Awaitable<TAuth | Unauthorized>;
  /** Folder for generated keys `<prefix>/<ulid><ext>`. Sanitized like every key. */
  prefix?: (context: KeyContext<TAuth, SchemaOutput<TSchema>>) => string;
  /** The whole key instead of `<prefix>/<ulid><ext>`; it must be unique. Sanitized like every key. */
  key?: (context: KeyContext<TAuth, SchemaOutput<TSchema>>) => string;
  /** "public" needs the storage's `publicUrl`. Default: "public" when the storage has one, otherwise "private". */
  visibility?: "public" | "private";
  /** Runs inside `/complete`, at least once per file; its JSON result reaches the client as `data`. */
  onComplete?: (context: CompleteContext<TAuth, SchemaOutput<TSchema>>) => Awaitable<TResult>;
}

/**
 * A route with its types erased, as the server runs it. Method syntax makes the typed callbacks assignable: route()
 * has already checked each one against its own context type.
 */
export interface RouteDefinition {
  accept: string | readonly string[];
  maxSize: Size;
  maxFiles?: number;
  metadata?: StandardSchema;
  visibility?: "public" | "private";
  authorize(context: AuthorizeContext<unknown>): Awaitable<unknown>;
  prefix?(context: KeyContext<object, unknown>): string;
  key?(context: KeyContext<object, unknown>): string;
  onComplete?(context: { request: Request; file: UploadedFile; metadata: unknown }): unknown;
}

export interface Route<TMetadataInput = unknown, TResult = unknown> {
  readonly definition: RouteDefinition;
  /** Type-only: never set at runtime. */
  readonly "~types"?: { readonly metadata: TMetadataInput; readonly result: TResult };
}

export function route<TAuth extends object, TSchema extends StandardSchema | undefined = undefined, TResult = null>(
  options: RouteOptions<TAuth, TSchema, TResult>,
): Route<SchemaInput<TSchema>, Jsonified<Awaited<TResult>>> {
  // The checker cannot relate onComplete's context while TAuth is generic (Jsonified<TAuth> stays deferred); the
  // handler builds exactly the contexts typed above.
  return { definition: options as RouteDefinition };
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/plugins.ts`:

```ts
// The server half of the plugin contract (spec §8). The plugins themselves arrive in M5; this file fixes the hooks they
// can use and the order and failure rules the handler applies to them.

import { CloudropError } from "../errors.ts";
import type { UploadedFile } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { StorageDriver } from "../storage/driver.ts";
import type { Awaitable, RouteDefinition } from "./route.ts";

/** A file the server has accepted and given a key, before any byte is uploaded. */
export interface PluginFile {
  id: string;
  key: string;
  name: string;
  size: number;
  type: string;
}

export interface BeforeUploadContext {
  request: Request;
  route: string;
  files: readonly PluginFile[];
  metadata: unknown;
  auth: unknown;
}

export interface AfterVerifyContext {
  request: Request;
  route: string;
  file: UploadedFile;
  metadata: unknown;
  auth: unknown;
  storage: StorageDriver;
}

export interface AbortContext {
  route: string;
  file: PluginFile;
  metadata: unknown;
  auth: unknown;
  /** "aborted": the client called /abort. "rejected": a check refused the file. "failed": the server could not start it. */
  reason: "aborted" | "rejected" | "failed";
}

export interface ServerHooks {
  /** Runs in `/upload` after authorization and policy, before anything is signed. Return an error to refuse every file. */
  beforeUpload?(context: BeforeUploadContext): Awaitable<CloudropError | undefined>;
  /** Runs in `/complete` after verification, before `onComplete`. An error deletes the object and reaches the client. */
  afterVerify?(context: AfterVerifyContext): Awaitable<CloudropError | undefined>;
  /** Runs for every file this plugin's `beforeUpload` accepted that will not complete (release a reservation here). */
  onAbort?(context: AbortContext): Awaitable<void>;
  /** Runs once per route in `createCloudrop`; throw a CloudropError with INVALID_CONFIG to refuse the route. */
  extendRoute?(route: { name: string; definition: RouteDefinition }): void;
}

export interface CloudropPlugin {
  id: string;
  server?: ServerHooks;
}

export interface Logger {
  error(message: string, error?: unknown): void;
  warn(message: string): void;
}

function refusal(plugin: CloudropPlugin, hook: string, value: unknown): CloudropError | undefined {
  if (value === undefined || value instanceof CloudropError) return value;
  throw new TypeError(`Plugin "${plugin.id}": ${hook} must return a CloudropError or nothing.`);
}

/** onAbort for each file on each plugin, in plugin order. A failing onAbort is logged and never stops the others. */
export async function runOnAbort(
  plugins: readonly CloudropPlugin[],
  contexts: readonly AbortContext[],
  logger: Logger,
): Promise<void> {
  for (const plugin of plugins) {
    for (const context of contexts) {
      try {
        await plugin.server?.onAbort?.(context);
      } catch (error) {
        logger.error(`cloudrop: plugin "${plugin.id}" failed in onAbort for file ${context.file.id}.`, error);
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * beforeUpload on each plugin in order. The first refusal, or the first throw, stops the rest; the plugins that had
 * already accepted get onAbort, so a reservation made by an earlier plugin is released.
 */
export async function runBeforeUpload(
  plugins: readonly CloudropPlugin[],
  context: BeforeUploadContext,
  logger: Logger,
): Promise<CloudropError | undefined> {
  for (const [index, plugin] of plugins.entries()) {
    const release = (reason: AbortContext["reason"]) =>
      runOnAbort(
        plugins.slice(0, index),
        context.files.map((file) => ({
          route: context.route,
          file,
          metadata: context.metadata,
          auth: context.auth,
          reason,
        })),
        logger,
      );
    let refused: CloudropError | undefined;
    try {
      refused = refusal(plugin, "beforeUpload", await plugin.server?.beforeUpload?.(context));
    } catch (error) {
      await release("failed");
      throw error;
    }
    if (refused) {
      await release("rejected");
      return refused;
    }
  }
  return undefined;
}

/** afterVerify on each plugin in order; the first refusal stops the rest. A throw escapes to the handler. */
export async function runAfterVerify(
  plugins: readonly CloudropPlugin[],
  context: AfterVerifyContext,
): Promise<CloudropError | undefined> {
  for (const plugin of plugins) {
    const refused = refusal(plugin, "afterVerify", await plugin.server?.afterVerify?.(context));
    if (refused) return refused;
  }
  return undefined;
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/config.ts`:

```ts
// createCloudrop's options, checked once at construction. Every mistake here throws INVALID_CONFIG with the option's
// name and the fix, so a misconfigured server fails at startup instead of on a user's first upload.

import { fromEnv } from "../env.ts";
import { configError } from "../errors.ts";
import type { StorageDriver } from "../storage/driver.ts";
import { formatBytes } from "../storage/validate.ts";
import { isAcceptEntry } from "./accept.ts";
import type { CloudropPlugin, Logger } from "./plugins.ts";
import type { Route, RouteDefinition } from "./route.ts";
import { parseSize, type Size } from "./size.ts";
import { importTokenKeys, type TokenKeys } from "./token.ts";

export interface CloudropOptions<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route>> {
  /** `r2({ bucket })` or `s3({ ... })`. */
  storage: StorageDriver;
  routes: TRoutes;
  /**
   * HMAC secrets for upload tokens, each at least 32 characters. The first signs; every one verifies, so put a new
   * secret first and keep the old one until its tokens expire (24 hours). Default: env CLOUDROP_SECRET.
   */
  secret?: string | readonly string[];
  /** Where the handler is mounted. Default "/api/cloudrop". */
  basePath?: string;
  /** Exact origins, such as "https://app.example.com", allowed to call the handler cross-origin with credentials. */
  trustedOrigins?: readonly string[];
  /** Files larger than this use multipart. Default 64 MiB. */
  multipartThreshold?: Size;
  plugins?: readonly CloudropPlugin[];
  /** Where failures inside the handler and configuration warnings go. Default: console. */
  logger?: Logger;
}

export interface RouteConfig {
  name: string;
  definition: RouteDefinition;
  accept: readonly string[];
  maxSize: number;
  maxFiles: number;
  visibility: "public" | "private";
}

export interface ServerConfig {
  storage: StorageDriver;
  routes: ReadonlyMap<string, RouteConfig>;
  tokenKeys: Promise<TokenKeys>;
  basePath: string;
  trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>;
  multipartThreshold: number | undefined;
  plugins: readonly CloudropPlugin[];
  logger: Logger;
}

export const ROUTE_NAME: RegExp = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/;
const BASE_PATH = /^(\/[A-Za-z0-9._~-]+)+$/;
const MIN_SECRET_LENGTH = 32;
const MAX_FILES_LIMIT = 100;
const DEFAULT_MAX_FILES = 10;

const isFunction = (value: unknown): boolean => typeof value === "function";

function readSecrets(secret: unknown): [string, ...string[]] {
  const secrets = secret === undefined ? [fromEnv("CLOUDROP_SECRET")].filter(Boolean) : [secret].flat();
  if (secrets.length === 0) {
    throw configError(
      "Missing the upload token secret. Set CLOUDROP_SECRET or pass secret to createCloudrop(); generate one with `openssl rand -base64 32`.",
    );
  }
  // The secret itself is never quoted: messages end up in logs.
  if (!secrets.every((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.length >= MIN_SECRET_LENGTH)) {
    throw configError(
      "Each upload token secret must be text of at least 32 characters. Generate one with `openssl rand -base64 32`.",
    );
  }
  return secrets as [string, ...string[]];
}

function readOrigins(origins: unknown): Set<string> {
  const list = origins ?? [];
  const valid =
    Array.isArray(list) &&
    list.every((origin) => typeof origin === "string" && URL.canParse(origin) && new URL(origin).origin === origin);
  if (!valid) {
    throw configError(
      'trustedOrigins must list exact origins such as "https://app.example.com": scheme and host, no path or trailing slash, and never "*".',
    );
  }
  return new Set(list);
}

function readRoute(name: string, route: Route, storage: StorageDriver): RouteConfig {
  const where = `Route "${name}"`;
  const definition = (route as Partial<Route> | null)?.definition;
  if (typeof definition !== "object" || definition === null) {
    throw configError(`${where} is not a route. Wrap its options in route({ ... }).`);
  }
  if (!isFunction(definition.authorize)) {
    throw configError(`${where} needs authorize. Use authorize: allowAnyone for a route anyone may upload to.`);
  }
  const accept = [definition.accept].flat();
  if (accept.length === 0 || !accept.every((entry) => typeof entry === "string" && isAcceptEntry(entry))) {
    throw configError(`${where}: accept takes MIME types in lower case, such as "image/png", "image/*" or "*/*".`);
  }
  const maxSize = parseSize(definition.maxSize);
  const limit = storage.rules.maxObjectSize;
  if (maxSize === null || maxSize < 1 || maxSize > limit) {
    throw configError(`${where}: maxSize must be between 1 byte and ${formatBytes(limit)}, such as 5242880 or "5MB".`);
  }
  const maxFiles = definition.maxFiles ?? DEFAULT_MAX_FILES;
  if (!Number.isInteger(maxFiles) || maxFiles < 1 || maxFiles > MAX_FILES_LIMIT) {
    throw configError(`${where}: maxFiles must be a whole number from 1 to ${MAX_FILES_LIMIT}.`);
  }
  if (definition.metadata !== undefined && !isFunction(definition.metadata?.["~standard"]?.validate)) {
    throw configError(`${where}: metadata must be a Standard Schema, such as a zod, valibot or arktype schema.`);
  }
  for (const option of ["prefix", "key", "onComplete"] as const) {
    if (definition[option] !== undefined && !isFunction(definition[option])) {
      throw configError(`${where}: ${option} must be a function.`);
    }
  }
  if (definition.prefix && definition.key) {
    throw configError(`${where}: pass prefix or key, not both. key builds the whole key; prefix only its folder.`);
  }
  const visibility = definition.visibility ?? (storage.publicUrl ? "public" : "private");
  if (visibility !== "public" && visibility !== "private") {
    throw configError(`${where}: visibility must be "public" or "private".`);
  }
  if (visibility === "public" && !storage.publicUrl) {
    throw configError(
      `${where} is public, but the storage has no publicUrl. Pass publicUrl to the storage preset, or make the route private.`,
    );
  }
  return { name, definition, accept, maxSize, maxFiles, visibility };
}

export function readConfig<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route>>(given: CloudropOptions<TRoutes>): ServerConfig {
  const options: Partial<CloudropOptions<TRoutes>> = given ?? {};
  const { storage } = options;
  if (!storage || !isFunction(storage.presignPut)) {
    throw configError("Pass storage to createCloudrop: r2({ bucket }) or s3({ ... }).");
  }
  const entries = Object.entries(options.routes ?? {});
  if (entries.length === 0) throw configError("Pass at least one route: routes: { avatar: route({ ... }) }.");
  const badName = entries.find(([name]) => !ROUTE_NAME.test(name));
  if (badName)
    throw configError("Route names may use letters, digits, - and _ (at most 64), such as avatar or post-image.");
  const basePath = options.basePath ?? "/api/cloudrop";
  if (typeof basePath !== "string" || !BASE_PATH.test(basePath)) {
    throw configError('basePath must be a path such as "/api/cloudrop": a leading slash, no trailing slash, no query.');
  }
  const multipartThreshold =
    options.multipartThreshold === undefined ? undefined : parseSize(options.multipartThreshold);
  if (multipartThreshold === null) {
    throw configError('multipartThreshold must be a whole number of bytes or a size such as "64MB".');
  }
  const plugins = options.plugins ?? [];
  const ids = plugins.map((plugin) => plugin?.id);
  if (!ids.every((id) => typeof id === "string" && id !== "") || new Set(ids).size !== ids.length) {
    throw configError("Every plugin needs its own id. Pass each plugin once.");
  }
  const logger = options.logger ?? console;
  const routes = new Map(entries.map(([name, route]) => [name, readRoute(name, route, storage)]));
  for (const route of routes.values()) {
    for (const plugin of plugins) plugin.server?.extendRoute?.({ name: route.name, definition: route.definition });
    if (route.visibility === "private" && storage.publicUrl) {
      logger.warn(
        `cloudrop: route "${route.name}" is private, but the storage has a publicUrl, so anyone who learns a key can read the file. Serve private files from a bucket without a public domain.`,
      );
    }
  }
  return {
    storage,
    routes,
    tokenKeys: importTokenKeys(readSecrets(options.secret)),
    basePath,
    trustedOrigins: readOrigins(options.trustedOrigins),
    multipartThreshold,
    plugins,
    logger,
  };
}

/** What every endpoint receives: the request (for authorize and hooks), its parsed JSON body and the configuration. */
export interface EndpointInput {
  request: Request;
  body: unknown;
  config: ServerConfig;
}
```

### Task 8: The handler and POST /upload

The heart of M2: `createCloudrop`, the request checks every endpoint shares (§7.2, §7.3), and `/upload` (§5, §5.1, §7.4,
§7.5). The handler starts with one endpoint; Tasks 9 to 11 add the others, one line each.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/cloudrop/src/server/http.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/presign.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/upload.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/handler.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/cloudrop.ts`
- Modify: `packages/cloudrop/src/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/cloudrop/src/testing/server-fixture.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/handler.test.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/server/upload.test.ts`, `packages/cloudrop/src/storage/presets.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 1 to 7; `planUpload` (`plan.ts`); `createFakeR2`, `r2`.
- Produces (`http.ts`): `type ResponseHeaders`; `isRecord(value): value is Record<string, unknown>`;
  `invalidInput(message): CloudropError`; `errorBody(error): ErrorBody`; `jsonResponse(body, status, headers?):
  Response`; `errorResponse(error, headers?): Response`; `storageFailure(error, logger): CloudropError`;
  `type OriginCheck`; `checkOrigin(request, trustedOrigins): OriginCheck`; `originNotAllowed(): CloudropError`;
  `preflight(request, trustedOrigins): Response`; `isJsonRequest(request): boolean`; `readJson(request):
  Promise<Result<unknown>>`.
- Produces (`presign.ts`): `TOKEN_LIFETIME_SECONDS` (86,400), `URL_LIFETIME_SECONDS` (900), `nowSeconds(): number`,
  `objectOptions(token)`, `presignSingle(storage, token): Promise<Result<SignedRequest>>`, `partCount(token:
  MultipartToken): number`, `partSizeOf(token: MultipartToken, partNumber: number): number`,
  `presignParts(storage, token: MultipartToken, partNumbers: readonly number[]): Promise<Result<SignedPart[]>>`.
- Produces: `handleUpload(input: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<UploadResponse>>`; `createHandler(config:
  ServerConfig): (request: Request) => Promise<Response>`; `interface Cloudrop<TRoutes> extends
  InferableCloudrop<…> { handler(request: Request): Promise<Response> }`; `createCloudrop<TRoutes>(options:
  CloudropOptions<TRoutes>): Cloudrop<TRoutes>`.
- Produces (test infrastructure, `src/testing/server-fixture.ts`): `SECRET`, `APP_ORIGIN`, `PUBLIC_URL`,
  `recordingLogger()`, `serverFixture(routes, options?)` returning `{ fake, storage, cloudrop, logger, storageCalls,
  post, single, multipart, send }`, `tokenPayload(token)`.
- Root exports gain `createCloudrop`, `route`, `allowAnyone`, `unauthorized` and the server types.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

All server tests go through `cloudrop.handler`, the entry point every adapter calls, on the fake R2, which enforces R2's
signed headers. Time is faked with `vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] })`: only `Date` moves, so WebCrypto promises
and the driver's timeouts are unaffected.

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/testing/server-fixture.ts`:

```ts
// A cloudrop server on the in-process fake R2, for the server's unit tests. Requests go through cloudrop.handler, the
// entry point every framework adapter calls.

import type { FileDescriptor, FileTicket, MultipartTicket, SingleTicket, UploadResponse } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { type Cloudrop, createCloudrop } from "../server/cloudrop.ts";
import type { CloudropOptions } from "../server/config.ts";
import type { Logger } from "../server/plugins.ts";
import type { Route } from "../server/route.ts";
import type { FetchLike, StorageDriver } from "../storage/driver.ts";
import { r2 } from "../storage/presets.ts";
import { createFakeR2, type FakeR2, type FakeR2Options } from "./fake-r2.ts";

export const SECRET = "test-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456789";
/** The origin the server is reached at; requests from it are same-origin. */
export const APP_ORIGIN = "https://app.example.com";
export const PUBLIC_URL = "https://cdn.example.com";

const credentials = { accessKeyId: "fake-access-key", secretAccessKey: "fake-secret-key" };

export interface RecordingLogger extends Logger {
  errors: { message: string; error: unknown }[];
  warnings: string[];
}

export function recordingLogger(): RecordingLogger {
  const errors: { message: string; error: unknown }[] = [];
  const warnings: string[] = [];
  return {
    errors,
    warnings,
    error: (message, error) => {
      errors.push({ message, error });
    },
    warn: (message) => {
      warnings.push(message);
    },
  };
}

export interface ServerFixture {
  fake: FakeR2;
  /** A driver on the same fake bucket, for reading and planting objects behind the server's back. */
  storage: StorageDriver;
  cloudrop: Cloudrop;
  logger: RecordingLogger;
  /** Every request the server sent to storage, as "METHOD path?query-names". */
  storageCalls: string[];
  /** POSTs JSON to an endpoint as a same-origin browser would. */
  post(endpoint: string, body: unknown, headers?: Record<string, string>): Promise<Response>;
  /** POSTs /upload with one file and returns its single-PUT ticket; anything else fails the test. */
  single(route: string, file: FileDescriptor): Promise<SingleTicket>;
  /** POSTs /upload with one file and returns its multipart ticket; anything else fails the test. */
  multipart(route: string, file: FileDescriptor): Promise<MultipartTicket>;
  /** Sends a presigned request to the fake bucket, as the browser would. */
  send(signed: { url: string; headers: Record<string, string> }, body: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer>): Promise<Response>;
}

export function serverFixture<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route>>(
  routes: TRoutes,
  options: Omit<Partial<CloudropOptions<TRoutes>>, "routes"> & {
    publicUrl?: boolean;
    bucketConfig?: FakeR2Options["bucketConfig"];
  } = {},
): ServerFixture {
  const { publicUrl, bucketConfig, ...rest } = options;
  const fake = createFakeR2({ bucket: "uploads", credentials, bucketConfig });
  const storageCalls: string[] = [];
  const recording: FetchLike = (input, init) => {
    const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
    const names = [...url.searchParams.keys()].filter((name) => !name.startsWith("X-Amz-"));
    storageCalls.push(`${init?.method ?? "GET"} ${url.pathname}${names.length > 0 ? `?${names.join("&")}` : ""}`);
    return fake.fetch(input, init);
  };
  const storage = r2({
    bucket: "uploads",
    accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    ...credentials,
    fetch: recording,
    publicUrl: publicUrl ? PUBLIC_URL : undefined,
  });
  const logger = recordingLogger();
  const cloudrop = createCloudrop({ storage, secret: SECRET, logger, routes, ...rest });
  const post = (endpoint: string, body: unknown, headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
    cloudrop.handler(
      new Request(`${APP_ORIGIN}/api/cloudrop/${endpoint}`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: APP_ORIGIN, ...headers },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      }),
    );
  async function ticket<TKind extends FileTicket["kind"]>(route: string, file: FileDescriptor, kind: TKind) {
    const response = await post("upload", { route, files: [file] });
    const [first] = ((await response.json()) as UploadResponse).files;
    if (first?.kind !== kind) throw new Error(`Expected a ${kind} ticket, got ${JSON.stringify(first)}`);
    return first as Extract<FileTicket, { kind: TKind }>;
  }
  return {
    fake,
    storage,
    cloudrop,
    logger,
    storageCalls,
    post,
    single: (route, file) => ticket(route, file, "single"),
    multipart: (route, file) => ticket(route, file, "multipart"),
    send: (signed, body) => fake.fetch(signed.url, { method: "PUT", headers: signed.headers, body }),
  };
}

/** The JSON payload of an upload token, read the way any client can read it. */
export function tokenPayload(token: string): Record<string, unknown> {
  const [body = ""] = token.split(".");
  return JSON.parse(
    new TextDecoder().decode(
      Uint8Array.from(atob(body.replaceAll("-", "+").replaceAll("_", "/")), (char) => char.charCodeAt(0)),
    ),
  );
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/handler.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { APP_ORIGIN, serverFixture } from "../testing/server-fixture.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route } from "./route.ts";

const TRUSTED = "https://admin.example.com";
const avatar = route({ accept: "image/png", maxSize: "1MB", authorize: allowAnyone });
const uploadBody = { route: "avatar", files: [{ name: "a.png", size: 10, type: "image/png" }] };

function setup() {
  return serverFixture({ avatar }, { trustedOrigins: [TRUSTED] });
}

function request(path: string, init: RequestInit & { headers?: Record<string, string> } = {}): Request {
  return new Request(`${APP_ORIGIN}${path}`, {
    method: "POST",
    body: JSON.stringify(uploadBody),
    ...init,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...init.headers },
  });
}

async function outcome(response: Response) {
  const body = (await response.json()) as { error?: { code: string } };
  return { status: response.status, code: body.error?.code };
}

describe("cloudrop.handler routing and request checks", () => {
  it.for<{
    name: string;
    path: string;
    init?: RequestInit & { headers?: Record<string, string> };
    status: number;
    code: string;
  }>([
    { name: "an unknown endpoint", path: "/api/cloudrop/delete", status: 404, code: "NOT_FOUND" },
    { name: "a path outside basePath", path: "/api/other/upload", status: 404, code: "NOT_FOUND" },
    // "/api/cloudropupload" starts with the base path's characters but is not under it.
    {
      name: "a path that only shares the base path's prefix",
      path: "/api/cloudropupload",
      status: 404,
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    },
    {
      name: "an inherited property name as endpoint",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/constructor",
      status: 404,
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
    },
    {
      name: "GET",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/upload",
      init: { method: "GET", body: null },
      status: 405,
      code: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED",
    },
    { name: "PUT", path: "/api/cloudrop/upload", init: { method: "PUT" }, status: 405, code: "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED" },
    // text/plain is CORS-safelisted: accepting it would let any site post here without a preflight.
    {
      name: "a text/plain body",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/upload",
      init: { headers: { "content-type": "text/plain" } },
      status: 415,
      code: "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
    },
    {
      name: "a form body",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/upload",
      init: { headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" } },
      status: 415,
      code: "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
    },
    {
      name: "a body that is not JSON",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/upload",
      init: { body: "{route:" },
      status: 400,
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
    },
    {
      name: "a body that is not UTF-8",
      path: "/api/cloudrop/upload",
      init: { body: new Uint8Array([0x22, 0xff, 0x22]) },
      status: 400,
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
    },
    { name: "an empty body", path: "/api/cloudrop/upload", init: { body: "" }, status: 400, code: "INVALID_INPUT" },
  ])("answers $name with $status $code", async ({ path, init, status, code }) => {
    expect(await outcome(await setup().cloudrop.handler(request(path, init)))).toEqual({ status, code });
  });

  it("names the allowed methods on 405", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { method: "GET", body: null }));
    expect(response.headers.get("allow")).toBe("POST, OPTIONS");
  });

  it("accepts application/json with a charset", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { headers: { "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8" } }),
    );
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("cloudrop.handler body cap (64 KiB)", () => {
  // Valid JSON of exactly `length` ASCII bytes, so only its size decides the answer. Unknown fields are ignored.
  const empty = JSON.stringify({ ...uploadBody, pad: "" });
  const sized = (length: number) => JSON.stringify({ ...uploadBody, pad: "x".repeat(length - empty.length) });

  it("reads a body of exactly 65,536 bytes", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { body: sized(65_536) }));
    expect(response.status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("refuses 65,537 bytes", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { body: sized(65_537) }));
    expect(await outcome(response)).toEqual({ status: 413, code: "BODY_TOO_LARGE" });
  });

  it("refuses a declared length over the cap without reading the body", async () => {
    let pulled = 0;
    const endless = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      pull(controller) {
        pulled += 1;
        controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(1024));
      },
    });
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", {
        body: endless,
        headers: { "content-length": "1000000" },
        duplex: "half",
      } as RequestInit & { headers: Record<string, string> }),
    );
    expect(await outcome(response)).toEqual({ status: 413, code: "BODY_TOO_LARGE" });
    // A stream may pull its first chunk eagerly; reading the body would pull at least 65 chunks.
    expect(pulled).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  it("stops reading a stream without a declared length once it passes the cap", async () => {
    let pulled = 0;
    const endless = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      pull(controller) {
        pulled += 1;
        controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(1024));
      },
    });
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { body: endless, duplex: "half" } as RequestInit & {
        headers?: Record<string, string>;
      }),
    );
    expect(await outcome(response)).toEqual({ status: 413, code: "BODY_TOO_LARGE" });
    expect(pulled).toBeLessThan(70);
  });
});

describe("cloudrop.handler origins and CORS", () => {
  it("accepts a same-origin request without CORS headers", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { headers: { origin: APP_ORIGIN } }),
    );
    expect({ status: response.status, cors: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") }).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      cors: null,
    });
  });

  it("accepts a request without Origin, which only a server or native app sends", async () => {
    expect((await setup().cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload"))).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("answers a trusted origin with its exact origin and credentials, never *", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { headers: { origin: TRUSTED } }));
    expect({
      status: response.status,
      origin: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin"),
      credentials: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-credentials"),
      vary: response.headers.get("vary"),
    }).toEqual({ status: 200, origin: TRUSTED, credentials: "true", vary: "Origin" });
  });

  it("keeps the CORS headers on an error answer, so the trusted page can read the error", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", {
        headers: { origin: TRUSTED },
        body: JSON.stringify({ route: "nope", files: [] }),
      }),
    );
    expect({ status: response.status, origin: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") }).toEqual({
      status: 404,
      origin: TRUSTED,
    });
  });

  it.for([
    { name: "another site", origin: "https://evil.example" },
    // Same host, other scheme: a different origin.
    { name: "the app over http", origin: "http://app.example.com" },
    { name: "a sandboxed frame (null)", origin: "null" },
  ])("refuses $name before any storage call", async ({ origin }) => {
    const fixture = setup();
    const response = await fixture.cloudrop.handler(request("/api/cloudrop/upload", { headers: { origin } }));
    expect({ ...(await outcome(response)), cors: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") }).toEqual({
      status: 403,
      code: "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED",
      cors: null,
    });
    expect(fixture.storageCalls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("answers a trusted preflight with POST and the requested headers", async () => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", {
        method: "OPTIONS",
        body: null,
        headers: {
          origin: TRUSTED,
          "access-control-request-method": "POST",
          "access-control-request-headers": "content-type, authorization",
        },
      }),
    );
    expect({
      status: response.status,
      origin: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin"),
      methods: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-methods"),
      headers: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-headers"),
      credentials: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-credentials"),
    }).toEqual({
      status: 204,
      origin: TRUSTED,
      methods: "POST",
      headers: "content-type, authorization",
      credentials: "true",
    });
  });

  it.for([
    { name: "an untrusted origin", origin: "https://evil.example", method: "POST" },
    { name: "a preflight for PUT", origin: TRUSTED, method: "PUT" },
  ])("refuses a preflight from $name without CORS headers", async ({ origin, method }) => {
    const response = await setup().cloudrop.handler(
      request("/api/cloudrop/upload", {
        method: "OPTIONS",
        body: null,
        headers: { origin, "access-control-request-method": method },
      }),
    );
    expect({ status: response.status, cors: response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") }).toEqual({
      status: 403,
      cors: null,
    });
  });
});

describe("cloudrop.handler failures", () => {
  it("answers a throwing callback with a generic 500 and logs the error itself", async () => {
    const secretDetail = new Error("db password=hunter2 at /srv/app/auth.ts:12");
    const failing = route({
      accept: "image/png",
      maxSize: "1MB",
      authorize: () => {
        throw secretDetail;
      },
    });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar: failing });
    const response = await fixture.post("upload", uploadBody);
    expect({ status: response.status, body: await response.json() }).toEqual({
      status: 500,
      body: {
        error: {
          code: "SERVER_ERROR",
          message: "The server could not handle the upload. Try again later.",
          retryable: false,
        },
      },
    });
    expect(fixture.logger.errors).toEqual([
      { message: "cloudrop: /upload failed on the server.", error: secretDetail },
    ]);
  });

  it("marks every answer as not cacheable, since it carries tokens and signed URLs", async () => {
    const response = await setup().post("upload", uploadBody);
    expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
  });
});
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/upload.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { CloudropError, fail } from "../errors.ts";
import type { FileTicket, MultipartTicket, SingleTicket, UploadResponse } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { MiB } from "../rules.ts";
import { r2 } from "../storage/presets.ts";
import { serverFixture, tokenPayload } from "../testing/server-fixture.ts";
import type { CloudropPlugin } from "./plugins.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route, unauthorized } from "./route.ts";
import type { StandardSchema } from "./schema.ts";

const NOW = new Date("2026-11-15T12:00:00Z");
const NOW_SECONDS = 1_794_744_000;
const ULID = /^[0-9A-HJKMNP-TV-Z]{26}$/;

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

const albumSchema: StandardSchema<{ albumId: string }> = {
  "~standard": {
    version: 1,
    vendor: "test",
    validate: (value) => {
      const albumId = (value as { albumId?: unknown } | null)?.albumId;
      return typeof albumId === "string"
        ? { value: { albumId } }
        : { issues: [{ message: "Expected a string", path: ["albumId"] }] };
    },
  },
};

const avatar = route({
  accept: ["image/png", "image/jpeg"],
  maxSize: "1MB",
  maxFiles: 2,
  metadata: albumSchema,
  authorize: ({ request, metadata }) =>
    request.headers.get("x-user")
      ? { userId: request.headers.get("x-user") as string, albumId: metadata.albumId }
      : unauthorized(),
  prefix: ({ userId, metadata }) => `users/${userId}/${metadata.albumId}`,
});
const files = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "100GB", authorize: allowAnyone });

const signedIn = { "x-user": "42" };
const png = (size = 1024, name = "me.png") => ({ name, size, type: "image/png" });

async function upload(fixture: ReturnType<typeof serverFixture>, body: unknown, headers = signedIn) {
  const response = await fixture.post("upload", body, headers);
  return {
    status: response.status,
    body: (await response.json()) as UploadResponse & { error?: { code: string; message: string } },
  };
}

describe("POST /upload: a single PUT ticket", () => {
  it("signs exactly the headers the bucket must store, with no Content-Length, and a token bound to the file", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar });
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "avatar", files: [png()], metadata: { albumId: "a1" } });
    const ticket = body.files[0] as SingleTicket;
    expect(status).toBe(200);
    expect(ticket.fileId).toMatch(ULID);
    expect(ticket).toEqual({
      kind: "single",
      fileId: ticket.fileId,
      key: `users/42/a1/${ticket.fileId}.png`,
      token: ticket.token,
      expiresAt: "2026-11-16T12:00:00.000Z",
      put: {
        url: ticket.put.url,
        headers: {
          "content-type": "image/png",
          "content-disposition": "inline; filename=\"me.png\"; filename*=UTF-8''me.png",
          "if-none-match": "*",
          "x-amz-meta-cloudrop-id": ticket.fileId,
        },
        expiresAt: "2026-11-15T12:15:00.000Z",
      },
    });
    expect(tokenPayload(ticket.token)).toEqual({
      v: 1,
      route: "avatar",
      fileId: ticket.fileId,
      key: ticket.key,
      name: "me.png",
      size: 1024,
      type: "image/png",
      owner: { userId: "42", albumId: "a1" },
      metadata: { albumId: "a1" },
      exp: NOW_SECONDS + 86_400,
    });
  });

  it("gives a URL the bucket accepts for exactly the declared bytes and headers, and nothing else", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "avatar", files: [png()], metadata: { albumId: "a1" } });
    const { put } = body.files[0] as SingleTicket;
    // Each refusal is R2's (spike rows 1, 2 and 18), reproduced by the fake.
    expect((await fixture.send(put, new Uint8Array(1023))).status).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await fixture.send({ ...put, headers: { ...put.headers, "content-type": "text/html" } }, new Uint8Array(1024)))
        .status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect(
      (await fixture.send({ ...put, headers: { ...put.headers, "x-amz-meta-owner": "me" } }, new Uint8Array(1024)))
        .status,
    ).toBe(403);
    expect((await fixture.send(put, new Uint8Array(1024))).status).toBe(200);
    // Create-only: the same URL cannot overwrite what it wrote.
    expect((await fixture.send(put, new Uint8Array(1024))).status).toBe(412);
  });

  it("ignores a key, size or type the client adds beside the descriptor", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "files",
      files: [{ name: "a.txt", size: 5, type: "text/plain", key: "../admin/a.txt", uploadId: "x" }],
    });
    const ticket = body.files[0] as SingleTicket;
    expect(ticket.key).toBe(`${ticket.fileId}.txt`);
  });

  it("stores a file the browser could not type as application/octet-stream, as an attachment", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "files", files: [{ name: "data", size: 0, type: "" }] });
    expect((body.files[0] as SingleTicket).put.headers).toMatchObject({
      "content-type": "application/octet-stream",
      "content-disposition": "attachment; filename=\"data\"; filename*=UTF-8''data",
    });
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: multipart tickets", () => {
  it("creates the upload with the object's headers and returns the plan and the first part URLs", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "files",
      files: [{ name: "big.bin", size: 8 * MiB + 1, type: "application/octet-stream" }],
    });
    const ticket = body.files[0] as MultipartTicket;
    expect({
      kind: ticket.kind,
      partSize: ticket.partSize,
      partCount: ticket.partCount,
      parts: ticket.parts.map(({ partNumber, headers, expiresAt }) => ({ partNumber, headers, expiresAt })),
    }).toEqual({
      kind: "multipart",
      partSize: 8 * MiB,
      partCount: 2,
      parts: [
        { partNumber: 1, headers: {}, expiresAt: "2026-11-15T12:15:00.000Z" },
        { partNumber: 2, headers: {}, expiresAt: "2026-11-15T12:15:00.000Z" },
      ],
    });
    expect(tokenPayload(ticket.token)).toMatchObject({ uploadId: "fake/upload+1==", partSize: 8 * MiB });
    expect(fixture.storageCalls).toEqual([`POST /uploads/${ticket.key}?uploads`]);
  });

  it("signs only the first 20 parts of a 25-part file", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "files",
      files: [{ name: "big.bin", size: 24 * 8 * MiB + 1, type: "application/octet-stream" }],
    });
    const ticket = body.files[0] as MultipartTicket;
    expect({ partCount: ticket.partCount, signed: ticket.parts.map((part) => part.partNumber) }).toEqual({
      partCount: 25,
      signed: Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => index + 1),
    });
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: authorization comes first", () => {
  it("refuses with authorize's message and starts nothing", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture(
      { files: route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: () => unauthorized("Log in first.") }) },
      { multipartThreshold: 0 },
    );
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "files",
      files: [{ name: "a.bin", size: 9 * MiB, type: "" }],
    });
    expect({ status, error: body.error }).toEqual({
      status: 401,
      error: { code: "UNAUTHORIZED", message: "Log in first.", retryable: false },
    });
    expect(fixture.storageCalls).toEqual([]);
  });

  // The authorize result rides in every token, which the browser can read: it must be a small JSON object.
  it.for<{ name: string; auth: unknown; log: string }>([
    { name: "null", auth: null, log: 'TypeError: Route "files": authorize must return an object or unauthorized().' },
    {
      name: "more than 2 KiB of JSON",
      auth: { record: "x".repeat(2048) },
      log: 'TypeError: Route "files": authorize returned more than 2 KiB of JSON. Return ids, not records.',
    },
  ])("answers 500 when authorize returns $name, and logs why", async ({ auth, log }) => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files: route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: () => auth as object }) });
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "files", files: [png()] });
    expect({ status, code: body.error?.code, logged: String(fixture.logger.errors[0]?.error) }).toEqual({
      status: 500,
      code: "SERVER_ERROR",
      logged: log,
    });
  });

  it("validates metadata before authorize sees it", async () => {
    const authorize = vi.fn(allowAnyone);
    const fixture = serverFixture({
      avatar: route({ accept: "image/png", maxSize: "1MB", metadata: albumSchema, authorize }),
    });
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "avatar", files: [png()], metadata: { albumId: 7 } });
    expect({ status, code: body.error?.code, calls: authorize.mock.calls.length }).toEqual({
      status: 400,
      code: "INVALID_METADATA",
      calls: 0,
    });
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: policy", () => {
  it("accepts a file of exactly maxSize and refuses one byte more, per file", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "avatar",
      files: [png(MiB, "exact.png"), png(MiB + 1, "over.png")],
      metadata: { albumId: "a1" },
    });
    expect(body.files.map((ticket: FileTicket) => (ticket.kind === "rejected" ? ticket.error : ticket.kind))).toEqual([
      "single",
      {
        code: "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
        message: "This file is over the 1 MiB limit. Pick a smaller file.",
        retryable: false,
      },
    ]);
  });

  it.for([
    {
      name: "a type the route does not accept",
      file: { name: "a.gif", size: 1, type: "image/gif" },
      message: "This upload does not accept image/gif files.",
    },
    {
      name: "an extension that contradicts the type",
      file: { name: "a.html", size: 1, type: "image/png" },
      message: "The file name ends in .html, which does not match its type image/png. Rename the file or pick another.",
    },
    {
      name: "a type with parameters",
      file: { name: "a.png", size: 1, type: "image/png; x=1" },
      message: "The file's type is not a valid MIME type. Pick another file.",
    },
  ])("refuses $name", async ({ file, message }) => {
    const { body } = await upload(serverFixture({ avatar }), {
      route: "avatar",
      files: [file],
      metadata: { albumId: "a1" },
    });
    expect(body.files[0]).toEqual({
      kind: "rejected",
      error: { code: "FILE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED", message, retryable: false },
    });
  });

  it("takes maxFiles files and refuses one more", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar });
    const two = await upload(fixture, { route: "avatar", files: [png(), png()], metadata: { albumId: "a1" } });
    const three = await upload(fixture, { route: "avatar", files: [png(), png(), png()], metadata: { albumId: "a1" } });
    expect([two.status, three.status, three.body.error]).toEqual([
      200,
      400,
      { code: "TOO_MANY_FILES", message: "This upload takes at most 2 files at once. Send fewer.", retryable: false },
    ]);
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: malformed requests from plain JavaScript", () => {
  const fileRule =
    'needs a name of 1 to 255 characters, a size in bytes and a type, such as { "name": "a.png", "size": 1024, "type": "image/png" }.';
  it.for<{ name: string; body: unknown; code: string; message: string }>([
    {
      name: "an array body",
      body: [],
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: "Send a JSON object: { route, files, metadata }.",
    },
    {
      name: "a numeric route",
      body: { route: 1, files: [png()] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: 'Name the upload route, such as { "route": "avatar" }.',
    },
    {
      name: "an unknown route",
      body: { route: "banner", files: [png()] },
      code: "UNKNOWN_ROUTE",
      message: 'There is no upload route named "banner". Check the route name.',
    },
    // A route name with a line break would forge a line in whatever logs the message.
    {
      name: "an unknown route with a line break",
      body: { route: "x\nERROR", files: [png()] },
      code: "UNKNOWN_ROUTE",
      message: "There is no upload route with that name. Check the route name.",
    },
    // A plain-object lookup would find Object.prototype here.
    {
      name: "an inherited property as route",
      body: { route: "__proto__", files: [png()] },
      code: "UNKNOWN_ROUTE",
      message: 'There is no upload route named "__proto__". Check the route name.',
    },
    {
      name: "no files",
      body: { route: "files", files: [] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: 'Send at least one file as { "name", "size", "type" }.',
    },
    {
      name: "files as an object",
      body: { route: "files", files: { 0: png() } },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: 'Send at least one file as { "name", "size", "type" }.',
    },
    {
      name: "a null file",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(), null] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 2 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a negative size",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(-1)] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a fractional size",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(1.5)] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a size as text",
      body: { route: "files", files: [{ name: "a", size: "10", type: "" }] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "an empty name",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(1, "")] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a 256-character name",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(1, "a".repeat(256))] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a name with a lone surrogate",
      body: { route: "files", files: [png(1, "a\ud800.png")] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
    {
      name: "a numeric type",
      body: { route: "files", files: [{ name: "a", size: 1, type: 5 }] },
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: `File 1 ${fileRule}`,
    },
  ])("refuses $name", async ({ body, code, message }) => {
    const result = await upload(serverFixture({ files }), body);
    expect(result.body.error).toEqual({ code, message, retryable: false });
  });

  it("counts a name's length in characters, so 255 emoji pass", async () => {
    // 255 code points are 510 UTF-16 code units: a .length check would refuse this name.
    const { status } = await upload(serverFixture({ files }), { route: "files", files: [png(1, "😀".repeat(255))] });
    expect(status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: keys", () => {
  it("normalizes the key the prefix built to NFC", async () => {
    const decomposed = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone, prefix: () => "cafe\u0301" });
    const { body } = await upload(serverFixture({ files: decomposed }), { route: "files", files: [png()] });
    const ticket = body.files[0] as SingleTicket;
    expect(ticket.key).toBe(`café/${ticket.fileId}.png`);
  });

  it("answers a prefix that would escape its folder with a generic 500 and logs why", async () => {
    // The prefix often holds user data (an id, a team name); a traversal there is a server bug, never a user's key.
    const escaping = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone, prefix: () => "users/../admin" });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files: escaping });
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "files", files: [png()] });
    expect({ status, code: body.error?.code }).toEqual({ status: 500, code: "SERVER_ERROR" });
    expect(String(fixture.logger.errors[0]?.error)).toBe(
      "TypeError: Route \"files\" built an unusable key: The object key contains a '.' or '..' segment. Use plain path segments.",
    );
  });
});

describe("POST /upload: storage failures and plugins", () => {
  it("rejects only the file whose upload could not start, without the storage's own message", async () => {
    const fake = serverFixture({ files }).fake;
    const storage = r2({
      bucket: "uploads",
      accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
      accessKeyId: "k",
      secretAccessKey: "s",
      fetch: fake.fetch,
    });
    const denied = new CloudropError(
      "STORAGE_DENIED",
      "The storage service refused the request. Check the access key.",
    );
    const failing = { ...storage, createMultipart: async () => fail(denied) };
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { storage: failing, multipartThreshold: MiB });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "files", files: [png(2 * MiB, "big.png"), png(10, "small.png")] });
    expect(body.files.map((ticket: FileTicket) => (ticket.kind === "rejected" ? ticket.error : ticket.kind))).toEqual([
      {
        code: "STORAGE_ERROR",
        message: "The storage service refused the request. Contact the site owner if this keeps happening.",
        retryable: false,
      },
      "single",
    ]);
    expect(fixture.logger.errors).toEqual([
      { message: "cloudrop: the storage service refused a request.", error: denied },
    ]);
  });

  it("lets a plugin refuse the request and releases what earlier plugins reserved", async () => {
    const events: string[] = [];
    const reserving: CloudropPlugin = {
      id: "reserving",
      server: {
        beforeUpload: ({ files: accepted }) => {
          events.push(`reserve ${accepted.length}`);
          return undefined;
        },
        onAbort: ({ reason, file }) => {
          events.push(`release ${reason} ${file.name}`);
        },
      },
    };
    const quota = new CloudropError("TOO_MANY_FILES", "You have no upload quota left.");
    const refusing: CloudropPlugin = {
      id: "refusing",
      server: { beforeUpload: () => quota, onAbort: () => void events.push("refusing released") },
    };
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { plugins: [reserving, refusing] });
    const { status, body } = await upload(fixture, { route: "files", files: [png(1, "a.png")] });
    expect({ status, error: body.error, events }).toEqual({
      status: 400,
      error: { code: "TOO_MANY_FILES", message: "You have no upload quota left.", retryable: false },
      events: ["reserve 1", "release rejected a.png"],
    });
  });

  it("hands beforeUpload only the accepted files, with their keys", async () => {
    const seen: { name: string; key: string }[] = [];
    const recorder: CloudropPlugin = {
      id: "recorder",
      server: {
        beforeUpload: ({ files: accepted }) => void seen.push(...accepted.map(({ name, key }) => ({ name, key }))),
      },
    };
    const fixture = serverFixture({ avatar }, { plugins: [recorder] });
    const { body } = await upload(fixture, {
      route: "avatar",
      files: [png(1, "ok.png"), png(1, "bad.gif")],
      metadata: { albumId: "a1" },
    });
    expect(seen).toEqual([{ name: "ok.png", key: (body.files[0] as SingleTicket).key }]);
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- `>=` instead of `>` at the 65,536-byte cap ("reads a body of exactly 65,536 bytes").
- Reading a declared 1 MB body before refusing it, or reading an endless stream to its end.
- Accepting `text/plain` or a form body: those need no preflight, so any site could post.
- Treating `http://app.example.com` as the `https` app, or `Origin: null` as trusted.
- Losing the CORS headers on an error, so a trusted page cannot read why it failed.
- Any storage call before `authorize` (`storageCalls` must be `[]`).
- An off-by-one at `maxSize` or `maxFiles`; refusing the whole request for one oversized file.
- Counting a name's length in UTF-16 code units (255 emoji are 510 units).
- A storage error message ("Check the access key…") reaching the browser.
- Not releasing an earlier plugin's reservation when a later plugin refuses.

'../server/cloudrop.ts' imported from …/src/testing/server-fixture.ts`, and "exports exactly the intended runtime
names" fails with `expected [ 'CloudropError', 'r2', 's3' ] to deeply equal [ Array(7) ]`.
