Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/http.ts`:

```ts
// What crosses the wire between the handler and the browser: response bodies, status codes, the origin and CORS rules
// (spec §7.2) and the body cap (spec §7.3).

import { CloudropError, type CloudropErrorCode, fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import { type ErrorBody, MAX_BODY_BYTES } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { Logger } from "./plugins.ts";

const STATUS = {
  INVALID_INPUT: 400,
  INVALID_CONFIG: 500,
  STORAGE_UNREACHABLE: 502,
  STORAGE_DENIED: 502,
  STORAGE_ERROR: 502,
  RATE_LIMITED: 503,
  NOT_FOUND: 404,
  ALREADY_EXISTS: 409,
  NO_SUCH_UPLOAD: 410,
  INVALID_PART: 400,
  CHECKSUM_MISMATCH: 400,
  UNAUTHORIZED: 401,
  ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED: 403,
  METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED: 405,
  UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE: 415,
  BODY_TOO_LARGE: 413,
  UNKNOWN_ROUTE: 404,
  INVALID_METADATA: 400,
  TOO_MANY_FILES: 400,
  FILE_TOO_LARGE: 400,
  FILE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED: 400,
  INVALID_TOKEN: 400,
  UPLOAD_EXPIRED: 410,
  NOT_UPLOADED: 409,
  PARTS_MISSING: 409,
  VERIFICATION_FAILED: 422,
  SERVER_ERROR: 500,
} satisfies Record<CloudropErrorCode, number>;

export type ResponseHeaders = Record<string, string>;

export const isRecord = (value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> =>
  typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);

export function invalidInput(message: string): CloudropError {
  return new CloudropError("INVALID_INPUT", message);
}

/** Only the code, message, retryable flag and part numbers leave the server: never a cause, stack or storage code. */
export function errorBody(error: CloudropError): ErrorBody {
  const body: ErrorBody = { code: error.code, message: error.message, retryable: error.retryable };
  if (error.parts) body.parts = [...error.parts];
  return body;
}

export function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status: number, headers: ResponseHeaders = {}): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    // Tokens and presigned URLs must never sit in a shared cache.
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "cache-control": "no-store", ...headers },
  });
}

export function errorResponse(error: CloudropError, headers: ResponseHeaders = {}): Response {
  return jsonResponse({ error: errorBody(error) }, STATUS[error.code], headers);
}

/**
 * A storage failure as the browser may see it: no storage code, no endpoint, no advice meant for the operator. The
 * full error goes to the logger.
 */
export function storageFailure(error: CloudropError, logger: Logger): CloudropError {
  logger.error("cloudrop: the storage service refused a request.", error);
  if (error.code === "RATE_LIMITED") {
    return new CloudropError("RATE_LIMITED", "The storage service is busy. Try again in a moment.", {
      retryable: true,
    });
  }
  return error.retryable
    ? new CloudropError("STORAGE_ERROR", "The storage service did not respond. Try again in a moment.", {
        retryable: true,
      })
    : new CloudropError(
        "STORAGE_ERROR",
        "The storage service refused the request. Contact the site owner if this keeps happening.",
      );
}

export type OriginCheck = { allowed: false } | { allowed: true; cors: ResponseHeaders };

/**
 * Same-origin requests and trustedOrigins pass. A request without Origin passes too: every browser sends Origin on a
 * POST, so its absence means a server or a native app, where cross-site request forgery does not apply. "null" (a
 * sandboxed frame or a file: page) never matches.
 */
export function checkOrigin(request: Request, trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>): OriginCheck {
  const origin = request.headers.get("origin");
  if (origin === null || origin === new URL(request.url).origin) return { allowed: true, cors: {} };
  if (!trustedOrigins.has(origin)) return { allowed: false };
  // The exact origin, never "*": the browser refuses "*" with credentials, and "*" would open the handler to every site.
  return {
    allowed: true,
    cors: { "access-control-allow-origin": origin, "access-control-allow-credentials": "true", vary: "Origin" },
  };
}

export const originNotAllowed = (): CloudropError =>
  new CloudropError(
    "ORIGIN_NOT_ALLOWED",
    "This site may not upload here. Add its origin to trustedOrigins in createCloudrop.",
  );

const HEADER_LIST = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]+(\s*,\s*[A-Za-z0-9-]+)*$/;

/** Answers a CORS preflight. A trusted origin may send the headers it asks for (an app's own auth header, say). */
export function preflight(request: Request, trustedOrigins: ReadonlySet<string>): Response {
  const verdict = checkOrigin(request, trustedOrigins);
  if (!verdict.allowed || request.headers.get("access-control-request-method") !== "POST") {
    return errorResponse(originNotAllowed());
  }
  const requested = request.headers.get("access-control-request-headers") ?? "";
  return new Response(null, {
    status: 204,
    headers: {
      ...verdict.cors,
      "access-control-allow-methods": "POST",
      "access-control-allow-headers": HEADER_LIST.test(requested) ? requested : "content-type",
      "access-control-max-age": "600",
      vary: "Origin, Access-Control-Request-Headers",
    },
  });
}

/** application/json is not a CORS-safelisted type, so a cross-origin page must preflight before it can post here. */
export function isJsonRequest(request: Request): boolean {
  return /^application\/json\s*(;|$)/i.test(request.headers.get("content-type") ?? "");
}

const bodyTooLarge = (): CloudropError =>
  new CloudropError("BODY_TOO_LARGE", "The request is larger than 64 KiB. Send fewer files at once.");

/** The parsed JSON body, read up to MAX_BODY_BYTES; a longer body is cancelled, not read to the end. */
export async function readJson(request: Request): Promise<Result<unknown>> {
  if (Number(request.headers.get("content-length")) > MAX_BODY_BYTES) return fail(bodyTooLarge());
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let length = 0;
  try {
    const reader = request.body?.getReader();
    for (let chunk = await reader?.read(); chunk && !chunk.done; chunk = await reader?.read()) {
      length += chunk.value.length;
      if (length > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
        await reader?.cancel();
        return fail(bodyTooLarge());
      }
      chunks.push(chunk.value);
    }
  } catch {
    return fail(invalidInput("The request body could not be read. Send the request again."));
  }
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(length);
  let offset = 0;
  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    bytes.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.length;
  }
  try {
    return ok(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes)));
  } catch {
    return fail(invalidInput("The request body is not valid JSON. Send a JSON object."));
  }
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/presign.ts`:

```ts
// Signing the URLs a client uploads to, for `/upload` and `/sign`. Everything comes from the verified token, never
// from the request: the client cannot change a key, size, type or part boundary.

import { fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import type { SignedPart, SignedRequest } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { PresignedRequest, StorageDriver } from "../storage/driver.ts";
import { contentDisposition } from "./key.ts";
import type { MultipartToken, UploadTokenPayload } from "./token.ts";

/** How long a client may resume an upload (spec §5.2). */
export const TOKEN_LIFETIME_SECONDS = 86_400;
/** How long one signed URL lives (spec §5.2); `/sign` renews it. */
export const URL_LIFETIME_SECONDS = 900;

export const nowSeconds = (): number => Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);

/** A URL never outlives the token it was signed for. The caller has checked that the token has not expired. */
const urlLifetime = (token: UploadTokenPayload): number => Math.min(URL_LIFETIME_SECONDS, token.exp - nowSeconds());

function toWire(signed: PresignedRequest): SignedRequest {
  return { url: signed.url, headers: signed.headers, expiresAt: signed.expiresAt.toISOString() };
}

/** The stored object's headers. createMultipart and presignPut sign the same ones, so both paths store the same object. */
export function objectOptions(token: UploadTokenPayload): {
  type: string;
  disposition: string;
  metadata: Record<string, string>;
} {
  return {
    type: token.type,
    disposition: contentDisposition(token.type, token.name),
    metadata: { "cloudrop-id": token.fileId },
  };
}

export async function presignSingle(storage: StorageDriver, token: UploadTokenPayload): Promise<Result<SignedRequest>> {
  const signed = await storage.presignPut(token.key, {
    ...objectOptions(token),
    size: token.size,
    // Create-only: a URL can never overwrite, and a retried PUT that gets 412 means the first one landed (spec §5.4).
    ifNoneMatch: true,
    expiresIn: urlLifetime(token),
  });
  return signed.error ? signed : ok(toWire(signed.data));
}

export function partCount(token: MultipartToken): number {
  return Math.ceil(token.size / token.partSize);
}

/** Every part but the last has exactly partSize bytes, so R2's equal-part rule holds by construction (spec §5.1). */
export function partSizeOf(token: MultipartToken, partNumber: number): number {
  const count = partCount(token);
  return partNumber < count ? token.partSize : token.size - (count - 1) * token.partSize;
}

/**
 * Signs the parts in parallel. Measured 2026-11-15 (Ryzen 9 9950X3D, 500 batches of 20 part URLs): 0.26 ms per batch
 * with Promise.all against 0.38 ms one after another on Bun 1.4.4, 0.39 against 0.57 ms on Node 26.13.
 */
export async function presignParts(
  storage: StorageDriver,
  token: MultipartToken,
  partNumbers: readonly number[],
): Promise<Result<SignedPart[]>> {
  const expiresIn = urlLifetime(token);
  const signed = await Promise.all(
    partNumbers.map(async (partNumber): Promise<Result<SignedPart>> => {
      const result = await storage.presignPart(token.key, token.uploadId, partNumber, {
        size: partSizeOf(token, partNumber),
        expiresIn,
      });
      return result.error ? result : ok({ partNumber, ...toWire(result.data) });
    }),
  );
  const parts: SignedPart[] = [];
  for (const result of signed) {
    if (result.error) return fail(result.error);
    parts.push(result.data);
  }
  return ok(parts);
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/upload.ts`:

```ts
// POST /upload (spec §5): bounds, metadata, authorize, policy, plugins, then one ticket per file. Nothing is signed
// before authorize has passed and the policy has accepted the file.

import { CloudropError, fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import { planUpload } from "../plan.ts";
import {
  type FileDescriptor,
  type FileTicket,
  MAX_NAME_LENGTH,
  PARTS_PER_SIGN,
  type UploadResponse,
} from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { formatBytes } from "../storage/validate.ts";
import { matchAccept, normalizeType } from "./accept.ts";
import { type EndpointInput, ROUTE_NAME, type RouteConfig, type ServerConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { errorBody, invalidInput, isRecord, storageFailure } from "./http.ts";
import { generatedKey, newFileId, normalizeKey } from "./key.ts";
import { type AbortContext, type PluginFile, runBeforeUpload, runOnAbort } from "./plugins.ts";
import { nowSeconds, objectOptions, presignParts, presignSingle, TOKEN_LIFETIME_SECONDS } from "./presign.ts";
import { isUnauthorized } from "./route.ts";
import { MAX_METADATA_JSON_BYTES, validateMetadata } from "./schema.ts";
import { type MultipartToken, signToken, type UploadTokenPayload } from "./token.ts";

const isDescriptor = (file: unknown): file is FileDescriptor =>
  isRecord(file) &&
  typeof file.name === "string" &&
  file.name.isWellFormed() &&
  file.name.length > 0 &&
  [...file.name].length <= MAX_NAME_LENGTH &&
  typeof file.size === "number" &&
  Number.isSafeInteger(file.size) &&
  file.size >= 0 &&
  typeof file.type === "string" &&
  file.type.length <= 255;

function readRequest(
  body: unknown,
  config: ServerConfig,
): Result<{ route: RouteConfig; files: FileDescriptor[]; metadata: unknown }> {
  if (!isRecord(body)) return fail(invalidInput("Send a JSON object: { route, files, metadata }."));
  if (typeof body.route !== "string")
    return fail(invalidInput('Name the upload route, such as { "route": "avatar" }.'));
  const route = config.routes.get(body.route);
  if (!route) {
    // The name is echoed only when it is a plausible route name: it comes from the client.
    const named = ROUTE_NAME.test(body.route) ? ` named "${body.route}"` : " with that name";
    return fail(new CloudropError("UNKNOWN_ROUTE", `There is no upload route${named}. Check the route name.`));
  }
  const { files } = body;
  if (!Array.isArray(files) || files.length === 0) {
    return fail(invalidInput('Send at least one file as { "name", "size", "type" }.'));
  }
  if (files.length > route.maxFiles) {
    return fail(
      new CloudropError("TOO_MANY_FILES", `This upload takes at most ${route.maxFiles} files at once. Send fewer.`),
    );
  }
  const bad = files.findIndex((file) => !isDescriptor(file));
  if (bad >= 0) {
    return fail(
      invalidInput(
        `File ${bad + 1} needs a name of 1 to ${MAX_NAME_LENGTH} characters, a size in bytes and a type, such as { "name": "a.png", "size": 1024, "type": "image/png" }.`,
      ),
    );
  }
  return ok({ route, files, metadata: body.metadata });
}

/** The file's key and stored type, or why the route refuses it. */
function prepareFile(
  route: RouteConfig,
  descriptor: FileDescriptor,
  context: { request: Request; metadata: unknown; auth: object },
): PluginFile | CloudropError {
  const type = normalizeType(descriptor.type);
  if (type === null) {
    return new CloudropError("FILE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED", "The file's type is not a valid MIME type. Pick another file.");
  }
  if (descriptor.size > route.maxSize) {
    return new CloudropError(
      "FILE_TOO_LARGE",
      `This file is over the ${formatBytes(route.maxSize)} limit. Pick a smaller file.`,
    );
  }
  const name = descriptor.name.normalize("NFC");
  const match = matchAccept(route.accept, type, name);
  if ("problem" in match) return new CloudropError("FILE_TYPE_NOT_ALLOWED", match.problem);
  const id = newFileId();
  const keyContext = {
    ...context.auth,
    request: context.request,
    metadata: context.metadata,
    file: { id, name, size: descriptor.size, type, ext: match.ext },
  };
  const { definition } = route;
  const raw = definition.key
    ? definition.key(keyContext)
    : generatedKey(definition.prefix?.(keyContext) ?? "", id, match.ext);
  const key = normalizeKey(raw);
  // The key comes from the application's prefix or key function, so a bad one is a server bug, not the user's.
  if ("problem" in key) throw new TypeError(`Route "${route.name}" built an unusable key: ${key.problem}`);
  return { id, key: key.key, name, size: descriptor.size, type };
}

/** One file's ticket: create the multipart upload if needed, sign the first URLs and the token. */
async function startFile(
  config: ServerConfig,
  base: Omit<UploadTokenPayload, "uploadId" | "partSize">,
): Promise<FileTicket> {
  const { storage, logger } = config;
  const { signing } = await config.tokenKeys;
  const expiresAt = new Date(base.exp * 1000).toISOString();
  const ticket = { fileId: base.fileId, key: base.key, expiresAt };
  const rejected = (error: CloudropError): FileTicket => ({
    kind: "rejected",
    error: errorBody(storageFailure(error, logger)),
  });
  const plan = planUpload(base.size, storage.rules, { multipartThreshold: config.multipartThreshold });
  if (plan.kind === "single") {
    const [put, token] = await Promise.all([presignSingle(storage, base), signToken(base, signing)]);
    return put.error ? rejected(put.error) : { kind: "single", ...ticket, token, put: put.data };
  }
  const created = await storage.createMultipart(base.key, objectOptions(base));
  if (created.error) return rejected(created.error);
  const token: MultipartToken = { ...base, uploadId: created.data.uploadId, partSize: plan.partSize };
  const first = Array.from({ length: Math.min(PARTS_PER_SIGN, plan.partCount) }, (_, index) => index + 1);
  const [parts, signed] = await Promise.all([presignParts(storage, token, first), signToken(token, signing)]);
  if (parts.error) {
    await storage.abortMultipart(token.key, token.uploadId);
    return rejected(parts.error);
  }
  return {
    kind: "multipart",
    ...ticket,
    token: signed,
    partSize: plan.partSize,
    partCount: plan.partCount,
    parts: parts.data,
  };
}

export async function handleUpload({ request, body, config }: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<UploadResponse>> {
  const parsed = readRequest(body, config);
  if (parsed.error) return parsed;
  const { route, files } = parsed.data;
  const metadata = await validateMetadata(route.definition.metadata, parsed.data.metadata);
  if (metadata.error) return metadata;
  const auth = await route.definition.authorize({ request, metadata: metadata.data });
  if (isUnauthorized(auth)) return fail(new CloudropError("UNAUTHORIZED", auth.message));
  if (typeof auth !== "object" || auth === null) {
    throw new TypeError(`Route "${route.name}": authorize must return an object or unauthorized().`);
  }
  // The result rides in every token; JSON.stringify throws for a BigInt or a cycle, which the handler reports.
  if (new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(auth)).length > MAX_METADATA_JSON_BYTES) {
    throw new TypeError(`Route "${route.name}": authorize returned more than 2 KiB of JSON. Return ids, not records.`);
  }
  const prepared = files.map((file) => prepareFile(route, file, { request, metadata: metadata.data, auth }));
  const accepted = prepared.filter((file): file is PluginFile => !(file instanceof CloudropError));
  const { plugins } = config;
  if (accepted.length > 0) {
    const refused = await runBeforeUpload(
      plugins,
      { request, route: route.name, files: accepted, metadata: metadata.data, auth },
      config.logger,
    );
    if (refused) return fail(refused);
  }
  const exp = nowSeconds() + TOKEN_LIFETIME_SECONDS;
  const tickets = await Promise.all(
    prepared.map((file): FileTicket | Promise<FileTicket> =>
      file instanceof CloudropError
        ? { kind: "rejected", error: errorBody(file) }
        : startFile(config, {
            v: 1,
            route: route.name,
            fileId: file.id,
            key: file.key,
            name: file.name,
            size: file.size,
            type: file.type,
            owner: auth,
            metadata: metadata.data,
            exp,
          }),
    ),
  );
  // Files that beforeUpload accepted but storage could not start: release what plugins reserved for them.
  const failed = prepared.flatMap((file, index): AbortContext[] =>
    file instanceof CloudropError || tickets[index]?.kind !== "rejected"
      ? []
      : [{ route: route.name, file, metadata: metadata.data, auth, reason: "failed" }],
  );
  await runOnAbort(plugins, failed, config.logger);
  return ok({ files: tickets });
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/handler.ts`:

```ts
// cloudrop.handler: Request in, Response out, never a throw. Routing, method, origin, content type and body cap are
// checked here once, before any endpoint runs.

import { CloudropError, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import type { Endpoint } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { EndpointInput, ServerConfig } from "./config.ts";
import {
  checkOrigin,
  errorResponse,
  isJsonRequest,
  jsonResponse,
  originNotAllowed,
  preflight,
  readJson,
} from "./http.ts";
import { handleUpload } from "./upload.ts";

type EndpointHandler = (input: EndpointInput) => Promise<Result<unknown>>;

const ENDPOINTS = {
  upload: handleUpload,
} satisfies Partial<Record<Endpoint, EndpointHandler>>;

function endpointOf(request: Request, basePath: string): keyof typeof ENDPOINTS | null {
  const { pathname } = new URL(request.url);
  if (!pathname.startsWith(`${basePath}/`)) return null;
  const name = pathname.slice(basePath.length + 1);
  // hasOwn, not `in`: "constructor" and "toString" are not endpoints.
  return Object.hasOwn(ENDPOINTS, name) ? (name as keyof typeof ENDPOINTS) : null;
}

export function createHandler(config: ServerConfig): (request: Request) => Promise<Response> {
  return async (request) => {
    const endpoint = endpointOf(request, config.basePath);
    if (endpoint === null) {
      return errorResponse(
        new CloudropError("NOT_FOUND", "There is no cloudrop endpoint at this path. Check basePath."),
      );
    }
    if (request.method === "OPTIONS") return preflight(request, config.trustedOrigins);
    if (request.method !== "POST") {
      return errorResponse(new CloudropError("METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED", "Send this request as POST."), {
        allow: "POST, OPTIONS",
      });
    }
    const origin = checkOrigin(request, config.trustedOrigins);
    if (!origin.allowed) return errorResponse(originNotAllowed());
    if (!isJsonRequest(request)) {
      return errorResponse(
        new CloudropError(
          "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
          "Send the request body as JSON with Content-Type: application/json.",
        ),
        origin.cors,
      );
    }
    const body = await readJson(request);
    if (body.error) return errorResponse(body.error, origin.cors);
    try {
      const result = await ENDPOINTS[endpoint]({ request, body: body.data, config });
      return result.error ? errorResponse(result.error, origin.cors) : jsonResponse(result.data, 200, origin.cors);
    } catch (error) {
      // A throw here is a bug in the application's callbacks, a plugin, or cloudrop: the details go to the log only.
      config.logger.error(`cloudrop: /${endpoint} failed on the server.`, error);
      return errorResponse(
        new CloudropError("SERVER_ERROR", "The server could not handle the upload. Try again later."),
        origin.cors,
      );
    }
  };
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/cloudrop.ts`:

```ts
import type { InferableCloudrop } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { type CloudropOptions, readConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { createHandler } from "./handler.ts";
import type { Route } from "./route.ts";

type RouteTypeMap<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route>> = {
  [TName in keyof TRoutes & string]: TRoutes[TName] extends Route<infer TMetadata, infer TResult>
    ? { metadata: TMetadata; result: TResult }
    : never;
};

export interface Cloudrop<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route> = Record<string, Route>>
  extends InferableCloudrop<RouteTypeMap<TRoutes>> {
  /** The single adapter surface: mount it at `basePath` in any framework that speaks Request and Response. */
  handler(request: Request): Promise<Response>;
}

/** Checks every option now and throws INVALID_CONFIG for a mistake, so a misconfigured server fails at start-up. */
export function createCloudrop<TRoutes extends Record<string, Route>>(
  options: CloudropOptions<TRoutes>,
): Cloudrop<TRoutes> {
  const config = readConfig(options);
  return { handler: createHandler(config) };
}
```

`presignParts`'s comment carries numbers measured on 2026-11-15. Re-measure with a scratch script outside the

```ts
import { r2 } from "<repo>/packages/cloudrop/src/storage/presets.ts";
const driver = r2({ bucket: "uploads", accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", accessKeyId: "k", secretAccessKey: "s" });
const parts = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => i + 1);
const sign = (n: number) => driver.presignPart("users/42/video.bin", "upload-id", n, { size: 8 * 1024 * 1024, expiresIn: 900 });
for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) await Promise.all(parts.map(sign));
const N = 500;
for (const round of [1, 2]) {
  let t = performance.now();
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) for (const n of parts) await sign(n);
  const sequential = (performance.now() - t) / N;
  t = performance.now();
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) await Promise.all(parts.map(sign));
  const parallel = (performance.now() - t) / N;
  console.log(`round ${round}: 20 part URLs sequential ${sequential.toFixed(3)} ms, Promise.all ${parallel.toFixed(3)} ms`);
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/sign.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { SignedPart, SignedRequest } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { MiB } from "../rules.ts";
import { SECRET, serverFixture } from "../testing/server-fixture.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route } from "./route.ts";

const NOW = new Date("2026-11-15T12:00:00Z");
const files = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone });
// 3 parts of the 8 MiB plan: 8 MiB, 8 MiB and 5 bytes.
const BIG = { name: "big.bin", size: 16 * MiB + 5, type: "application/octet-stream" };
const SMALL = { name: "a.txt", size: 5, type: "text/plain" };

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

async function sign(fixture: ReturnType<typeof serverFixture>, body: unknown) {
  const response = await fixture.post("sign", body);
  return {
    status: response.status,
    body: (await response.json()) as {
      parts?: SignedPart[];
      put?: SignedRequest;
      error?: { code: string; message: string };
    },
  };
}

describe("POST /sign: multipart", () => {
  it("signs the requested parts at their planned sizes, which the bucket then accepts", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    const { body } = await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts: [3, 1] });
    const [last, first] = body.parts ?? [];
    expect([last?.partNumber, first?.partNumber]).toEqual([3, 1]);
    // The last part is signed for 5 bytes, the first for a full 8 MiB: R2 enforces the signed length.
    expect((await fixture.send(last as SignedPart, new Uint8Array(5))).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await fixture.send(last as SignedPart, new Uint8Array(6))).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await fixture.send(first as SignedPart, new Uint8Array(8 * MiB))).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("takes the key from the token, whatever else the body says", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    const { body } = await sign(fixture, {
      token: ticket.token,
      parts: [1],
      key: "victim/file.bin",
      uploadId: "other",
    });
    const url = new URL(body.parts?.[0]?.url ?? "");
    expect({ path: url.pathname, uploadId: url.searchParams.get("uploadId") }).toEqual({
      path: `/uploads/${ticket.key}`,
      uploadId: "fake/upload+1==",
    });
  });

  it("never signs a URL that outlives its token", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    // One minute before the token's 24 hours end, a 15-minute URL would outlive it by 14 minutes.
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-16T11:59:00Z"));
    const { body } = await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts: [1] });
    expect(body.parts?.[0]?.expiresAt).toBe("2026-11-16T12:00:00.000Z");
  });

  const rule = "Ask for 1 to 20 different part numbers, each from 1 to 3.";
  it.for<{ name: string; parts: unknown }>([
    { name: "no parts", parts: undefined },
    { name: "an empty list", parts: [] },
    { name: "part 0", parts: [0] },
    { name: "a part past the plan", parts: [4] },
    { name: "a repeated part", parts: [1, 1] },
    { name: "a fractional part", parts: [1.5] },
    { name: "a part as text", parts: ["1"] },
    { name: "parts as a number", parts: 1 },
  ])("refuses $name", async ({ parts }) => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    expect((await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts })).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: rule,
      retryable: false,
    });
  });

  it("signs 20 parts in one call and refuses 21", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", { ...BIG, size: 21 * 8 * MiB });
    const twenty = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, index) => index + 1);
    const ok = await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts: twenty });
    const tooMany = await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts: [...twenty, 21] });
    expect([ok.body.parts?.length, tooMany.body.error?.code]).toEqual([20, "INVALID_INPUT"]);
  });
});

describe("POST /sign: single PUT", () => {
  it("renews the URL with the same signed headers", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", SMALL);
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-15T12:20:00Z"));
    const { body } = await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token });
    expect({ headers: body.put?.headers, expiresAt: body.put?.expiresAt }).toEqual({
      headers: ticket.put.headers,
      expiresAt: "2026-11-15T12:35:00.000Z",
    });
    expect((await fixture.send(body.put as SignedRequest, new TextEncoder().encode("hello"))).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("refuses part numbers for a single PUT", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", SMALL);
    expect((await sign(fixture, { token: ticket.token, parts: [1] })).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "INVALID_INPUT",
      message: "This upload is a single PUT: send { token } without parts.",
      retryable: false,
    });
  });
});

describe("POST /sign: tokens", () => {
  it("answers an expired token with 410 UPLOAD_EXPIRED", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", SMALL);
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-16T12:00:00Z"));
    const response = await fixture.post("sign", { token: ticket.token });
    expect({
      status: response.status,
      code: ((await response.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code,
    }).toEqual({
      status: 410,
      code: "UPLOAD_EXPIRED",
    });
  });

  it("refuses a token signed with a secret this server does not hold", async () => {
    const ticket = await serverFixture({ files }, { secret: "another-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456" }).single(
      "files",
      SMALL,
    );
    const response = await serverFixture({ files }).post("sign", { token: ticket.token });
    expect([response.status, ((await response.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code]).toEqual([
      400,
      "INVALID_TOKEN",
    ]);
  });

  it.for<{ name: string; body: unknown; code: string }>([
    { name: "no token", body: {}, code: "INVALID_TOKEN" },
    { name: "a numeric token", body: { token: 5 }, code: "INVALID_TOKEN" },
    { name: "a body that is a string", body: "token", code: "INVALID_INPUT" },
  ])("answers $name with 400 $code", async ({ body, code }) => {
    const response = await serverFixture({ files }).post("sign", body);
    expect([response.status, ((await response.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code]).toEqual([400, code]);
  });

  it("accepts a token signed with the previous secret after a rotation", async () => {
    const before = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await before.single("files", SMALL);
    const after = serverFixture({ files }, { secret: ["rotated-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456", SECRET] });
    expect((await after.post("sign", { token: ticket.token })).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("refuses a token whose route was removed", async () => {
    const ticket = await serverFixture({ files }).single("files", SMALL);
    const redeployed = serverFixture({ other: files });
    expect(
      ((await (await redeployed.post("sign", { token: ticket.token })).json()) as { error: unknown }).error,
    ).toEqual({
      code: "UNKNOWN_ROUTE",
      message: "The upload route of this token no longer exists. Start a new upload.",
      retryable: false,
    });
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- Signing the last part at `partSize` instead of its real size: R2 would refuse the real bytes (403).
- Taking `key` or `uploadId` from the body ("takes the key from the token…").
- A 15-minute URL signed a minute before the token expires.
- Duplicated part numbers, part 0, a part past the plan, or more than 20 parts accepted.
- Only the first secret verifying after a rotation.
- A token kept valid after its route was removed by a deploy.

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/token-request.ts`:

```ts
import { CloudropError, fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import type { RouteConfig, ServerConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { invalidInput, isRecord } from "./http.ts";
import { nowSeconds } from "./presign.ts";
import { type UploadTokenPayload, verifyToken } from "./token.ts";

/**
 * The verified token of a `/sign`, `/complete` or `/abort` body and the route it was issued for. The route is looked
 * up again: a route removed by a deploy stops accepting its old tokens.
 */
export async function readTokenRequest(
  body: unknown,
  config: ServerConfig,
  options: { allowExpired?: boolean } = {},
): Promise<Result<{ token: UploadTokenPayload; route: RouteConfig }>> {
  if (!isRecord(body)) return fail(invalidInput('Send a JSON object with the upload token: { "token": "..." }.'));
  const verified = await verifyToken(body.token, (await config.tokenKeys).all, nowSeconds(), options);
  if (verified.error) return verified;
  const route = config.routes.get(verified.data.route);
  if (!route) {
    return fail(
      new CloudropError("UNKNOWN_ROUTE", "The upload route of this token no longer exists. Start a new upload."),
    );
  }
  return ok({ token: verified.data, route });
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/sign.ts`:

```ts
// POST /sign (spec §5.2): new URLs for an upload in progress, at most PARTS_PER_SIGN per call. The token decides the
// key, the sizes and the part count; the request only says which parts.

import { fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import { PARTS_PER_SIGN, type SignResponse } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { EndpointInput } from "./config.ts";
import { invalidInput, storageFailure } from "./http.ts";
import { partCount, presignParts, presignSingle } from "./presign.ts";
import { isMultipart } from "./token.ts";
import { readTokenRequest } from "./token-request.ts";

const isPartList = (parts: unknown, count: number): parts is number[] =>
  Array.isArray(parts) &&
  parts.length >= 1 &&
  parts.length <= PARTS_PER_SIGN &&
  new Set(parts).size === parts.length &&
  parts.every((part) => Number.isInteger(part) && part >= 1 && part <= count);

export async function handleSign({ body, config }: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<SignResponse>> {
  const request = await readTokenRequest(body, config);
  if (request.error) return request;
  const { token } = request.data;
  // readTokenRequest has checked that the body is an object.
  const { parts } = body as { parts?: unknown };
  if (!isMultipart(token)) {
    if (parts !== undefined) return fail(invalidInput("This upload is a single PUT: send { token } without parts."));
    const put = await presignSingle(config.storage, token);
    return put.error ? fail(storageFailure(put.error, config.logger)) : ok({ put: put.data });
  }
  const count = partCount(token);
  if (!isPartList(parts, count)) {
    return fail(invalidInput(`Ask for 1 to ${PARTS_PER_SIGN} different part numbers, each from 1 to ${count}.`));
  }
  const signed = await presignParts(config.storage, token, parts);
  return signed.error ? fail(storageFailure(signed.error, config.logger)) : ok({ parts: signed.data });
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `abortContext(token: UploadTokenPayload, reason: AbortContext["reason"]): AbortContext`
  (`token-request.ts`); `handleAbort(input: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<AbortResponse>>`; `/abort` routed;
  `forgeToken(token: string, changes: Record<string, unknown>): string` in the server fixture (a payload changed, the

```ts
/** The token with its payload changed and its signature kept: what an attacker can build without the secret. */
export function forgeToken(token: string, changes: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  const [, signature = ""] = token.split(".");
  const json = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ ...tokenPayload(token), ...changes }));
  const body = btoa(String.fromCharCode(...json))
    .replaceAll("+", "-")
    .replaceAll("/", "_")
    .replace(/=+$/, "");
  return `${body}.${signature}`;
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/abort.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { MiB } from "../rules.ts";
import { forgeToken, serverFixture } from "../testing/server-fixture.ts";
import type { AbortContext, CloudropPlugin } from "./plugins.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route } from "./route.ts";

const files = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: () => ({ userId: "42" }) });
const TEXT = { name: "hello.txt", size: 5, type: "text/plain" };

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-15T12:00:00Z"));
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

function recordingPlugin(calls: AbortContext[]): CloudropPlugin {
  return { id: "recorder", server: { onAbort: (context) => void calls.push(context) } };
}

describe("POST /abort", () => {
  it("aborts a multipart upload and tells the plugins, with the token's owner and file", async () => {
    const calls: AbortContext[] = [];
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0, plugins: [recordingPlugin(calls)] });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", {
      name: "big.bin",
      size: 9 * MiB,
      type: "application/octet-stream",
    });
    const response = await fixture.post("abort", { token: ticket.token });
    expect({ status: response.status, body: await response.json(), pending: fixture.fake.pendingUploads() }).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: { fileId: ticket.fileId },
      pending: 0,
    });
    expect(calls).toEqual([
      {
        route: "files",
        file: { id: ticket.fileId, key: ticket.key, name: "big.bin", size: 9 * MiB, type: "application/octet-stream" },
        metadata: undefined,
        auth: { userId: "42" },
        reason: "aborted",
      },
    ]);
  });

  it("deletes a single PUT that already landed", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    await fixture.send(ticket.put, new TextEncoder().encode("hello"));
    expect((await fixture.post("abort", { token: ticket.token })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps an object another writer stored under the key", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    await fixture.storage.put(ticket.key, "other", { metadata: { "cloudrop-id": "someone-else" } });
    expect((await fixture.post("abort", { token: ticket.token })).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data?.size).toBe(5);
  });

  it("accepts an expired token, so a cancelled upload can always be cleaned up", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", { name: "big.bin", size: 9 * MiB, type: "" });
    vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-20T12:00:00Z"));
    expect((await fixture.post("abort", { token: ticket.token })).status).toBe(200);
    expect(fixture.fake.pendingUploads()).toBe(0);
  });

  it("refuses a token rebound to another key and deletes nothing", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files: route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone }) });
    await fixture.storage.put("victim.txt", "keep me");
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    const response = await fixture.post("abort", { token: forgeToken(ticket.token, { key: "victim.txt" }) });
    expect([response.status, (await fixture.storage.head("victim.txt")).data?.size]).toEqual([400, 7]);
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- Deleting whatever sits at the key: another writer's object would go ("keeps an object another writer stored…").
- Refusing an expired token: an abandoned multipart upload could then only wait for the lifecycle rule.
- Trusting a rebound key: `victim.txt` would be deleted.
- `onAbort` not told, so a quota reservation would leak.

```ts
/** The onAbort context for a token's file. */
export function abortContext(token: UploadTokenPayload, reason: AbortContext["reason"]): AbortContext {
  const { fileId: id, key, name, size, type } = token;
  return {
    route: token.route,
    file: { id, key, name, size, type },
    metadata: token.metadata,
    auth: token.owner,
    reason,
  };
}
```

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/abort.ts`:

```ts
// POST /abort (spec §5.5): the user cancelled. Accepts an expired token, because cleaning up is always allowed.

import { fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import type { AbortResponse } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { EndpointInput } from "./config.ts";
import { storageFailure } from "./http.ts";
import { runOnAbort } from "./plugins.ts";
import { isMultipart } from "./token.ts";
import { abortContext, readTokenRequest } from "./token-request.ts";

export async function handleAbort({ body, config }: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<AbortResponse>> {
  const verified = await readTokenRequest(body, config, { allowExpired: true });
  if (verified.error) return verified;
  const { token } = verified.data;
  const { storage, logger } = config;
  if (isMultipart(token)) {
    const aborted = await storage.abortMultipart(token.key, token.uploadId);
    if (aborted.error) return fail(storageFailure(aborted.error, logger));
  } else {
    // A single PUT may already have landed. Delete only this upload's own object, never another writer's.
    const head = await storage.head(token.key);
    if (head.error) return fail(storageFailure(head.error, logger));
    if (head.data?.metadata["cloudrop-id"] === token.fileId) {
      const removed = await storage.delete(token.key);
      if (removed.error) return fail(storageFailure(removed.error, logger));
    }
  }
  await runOnAbort(config.plugins, [abortContext(token, "aborted")], logger);
  return ok({ fileId: token.fileId });
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { CloudropError } from "../errors.ts";
import type { CompleteResponse, SignedPart } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { MiB } from "../rules.ts";
import { PUBLIC_URL, serverFixture } from "../testing/server-fixture.ts";
import type { CloudropPlugin } from "./plugins.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route } from "./route.ts";

const files = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone });
const TEXT = { name: "hello.txt", size: 5, type: "text/plain" };
const BIG = { name: "big.bin", size: 8 * MiB + 5, type: "application/octet-stream" };
const hello = new TextEncoder().encode("hello");

beforeEach(() => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["Date"] });
  vi.setSystemTime(new Date("2026-11-15T12:00:00Z"));
});
afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

type Fixture = ReturnType<typeof serverFixture>;

async function complete(fixture: Fixture, token: string) {
  const response = await fixture.post("complete", { token });
  return {
    status: response.status,
    body: (await response.json()) as CompleteResponse & { error?: Record<string, unknown> },
  };
}

describe("POST /complete: single PUT", () => {
  it("verifies the stored object and returns the file and onComplete's JSON result", async () => {
    const received: unknown[] = [];
    const profile = route({
      accept: "text/plain",
      maxSize: "1KB",
      authorize: () => ({ userId: "42", since: new Date("2026-01-01T00:00:00Z") }),
      onComplete: ({ userId, since, file, metadata }) => {
        received.push({ userId, since, metadata });
        return { stored: file.key, at: new Date("2026-11-15T12:00:01Z") };
      },
    });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ profile });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("profile", TEXT);
    expect((await fixture.send(ticket.put, hello)).status).toBe(200);
    expect(await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).toEqual({
      status: 200,
      body: {
        file: { id: ticket.fileId, key: ticket.key, name: "hello.txt", size: 5, type: "text/plain", url: null },
        data: { stored: ticket.key, at: "2026-11-15T12:00:01.000Z" },
      },
    });
    // The authorize result came back from the token: its Date is now the JSON string, as the types say.
    expect(received).toEqual([{ userId: "42", since: "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", metadata: undefined }]);
  });

  it("gives a public route's file its percent-encoded public URL", async () => {
    const shared = route({
      accept: "*/*",
      maxSize: "1GB",
      authorize: allowAnyone,
      visibility: "public",
      prefix: () => "team files",
    });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ shared }, { publicUrl: true });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("shared", TEXT);
    await fixture.send(ticket.put, hello);
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.file.url).toBe(
      `${PUBLIC_URL}/team%20files/${ticket.fileId}.txt`,
    );
  });

  it("answers 409 NOT_UPLOADED before the bytes are there", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    expect(await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).toEqual({
      status: 409,
      body: {
        error: {
          code: "NOT_UPLOADED",
          message: "The file has not been uploaded yet. Upload it, then complete the upload.",
          retryable: false,
        },
      },
    });
  });

  it("completes again when retried, running onComplete each time (at-least-once)", async () => {
    let calls = 0;
    const counted = route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: "1GB", authorize: allowAnyone, onComplete: () => ++calls });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ counted });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("counted", TEXT);
    await fixture.send(ticket.put, hello);
    const first = await complete(fixture, ticket.token);
    const second = await complete(fixture, ticket.token);
    expect([first.body.data, second.body.data, second.body.file.id]).toEqual([1, 2, ticket.fileId]);
  });

  // A backend that did not enforce the signed headers (R2 does: spike rows 1 and 2) stored the wrong object under the
  // upload's own id. Both checks are needed: either one alone lets the other mismatch through.
  it.for([
    { name: "a different type", body: "<svg>", type: "text/html" },
    { name: "a different size", body: "hello!", type: "text/plain" },
  ])("discards an object with $name and answers VERIFICATION_FAILED", async ({ body, type }) => {
    const aborted: string[] = [];
    const plugin: CloudropPlugin = { id: "p", server: { onAbort: ({ reason }) => void aborted.push(reason) } };
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { plugins: [plugin] });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    await fixture.storage.put(ticket.key, body, { type, metadata: { "cloudrop-id": ticket.fileId } });
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "VERIFICATION_FAILED",
      message: "The uploaded file does not match what was declared, so it was discarded. Upload it again.",
      retryable: false,
    });
    expect([(await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data, aborted]).toEqual([null, ["rejected"]]);
  });

  it("never deletes an object another writer stored under the key", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    await fixture.storage.put(ticket.key, "hello", { type: "text/plain", metadata: { "cloudrop-id": "someone-else" } });
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error?.code).toBe("VERIFICATION_FAILED");
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data?.metadata).toEqual({ "cloudrop-id": "someone-else" });
  });
});

describe("POST /complete: multipart", () => {
  const partOne = new Uint8Array(8 * MiB);
  const partTwo = new Uint8Array(5).fill(7);

  it("lists the missing parts, then completes once they arrive", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    expect((await fixture.send(ticket.parts[0] as SignedPart, partOne)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "PARTS_MISSING",
      message: "1 of 2 parts are missing or have the wrong size. Upload them again, then complete the upload.",
      retryable: false,
      parts: [2],
    });
    expect((await fixture.send(ticket.parts[1] as SignedPart, partTwo)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data).toMatchObject({
      size: 8 * MiB + 5,
      type: "application/octet-stream",
      metadata: { "cloudrop-id": ticket.fileId },
    });
    expect((await fixture.storage.getRange(ticket.key, 8 * MiB, 8 * MiB + 4)).data).toEqual(partTwo);
  });

  it("asks again for a part stored at the wrong size instead of letting completion fail", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    const uploadId = "fake/upload+1==";
    await fixture.send(ticket.parts[0] as SignedPart, partOne);
    // Signed for 4 bytes by someone holding the storage credentials: the plan says 5.
    const wrong = await fixture.storage.presignPart(ticket.key, uploadId, 2, { size: 4, expiresIn: 60 });
    if (wrong.error) throw wrong.error;
    await fixture.send(wrong.data, new Uint8Array(4));
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error?.parts).toEqual([2]);
  });

  it("completes again when retried after the upload finished, by checking the stored object", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    await fixture.send(ticket.parts[0] as SignedPart, partOne);
    await fixture.send(ticket.parts[1] as SignedPart, partTwo);
    const first = await complete(fixture, ticket.token);
    const retried = await complete(fixture, ticket.token);
    expect([first.status, retried.status, retried.body.file.id]).toEqual([200, 200, ticket.fileId]);
  });

  it("answers 410 NO_SUCH_UPLOAD for an aborted upload", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    await fixture.post("abort", { token: ticket.token });
    expect(await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).toEqual({
      status: 410,
      body: {
        error: {
          code: "NO_SUCH_UPLOAD",
          message: "This upload was aborted or has expired. Start it again.",
          retryable: false,
        },
      },
    });
  });

  it("refuses to complete over an object that appeared at the key, and keeps that object", async () => {
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { multipartThreshold: 0 });
    const ticket = await fixture.multipart("files", BIG);
    await fixture.send(ticket.parts[0] as SignedPart, partOne);
    await fixture.send(ticket.parts[1] as SignedPart, partTwo);
    await fixture.storage.put(ticket.key, "first");
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "ALREADY_EXISTS",
      message: "Another file already has this upload's key. Start a new upload.",
      retryable: false,
    });
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data?.size).toBe(5);
    expect(fixture.fake.pendingUploads()).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe("POST /complete: plugins and onComplete", () => {
  it("deletes the object when afterVerify refuses it and returns the plugin's error", async () => {
    const refusal = new CloudropError("VERIFICATION_FAILED", "This file looks like an executable. Upload a document.");
    const checker: CloudropPlugin = { id: "checker", server: { afterVerify: () => refusal } };
    const fixture = serverFixture({ files }, { plugins: [checker] });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("files", TEXT);
    await fixture.send(ticket.put, hello);
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "VERIFICATION_FAILED",
      message: "This file looks like an executable. Upload a document.",
      retryable: false,
    });
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps the verified object when onComplete throws, and asks the client to retry", async () => {
    const failure = new Error("database unavailable");
    const failing = route({
      accept: "*/*",
      maxSize: "1GB",
      authorize: allowAnyone,
      onComplete: () => {
        throw failure;
      },
    });
    const fixture = serverFixture({ failing });
    const ticket = await fixture.single("failing", TEXT);
    await fixture.send(ticket.put, hello);
    expect((await complete(fixture, ticket.token)).body.error).toEqual({
      code: "SERVER_ERROR",
      message: "The file was uploaded, but the server could not finish processing it. Try again in a moment.",
      retryable: true,
    });
    expect((await fixture.storage.head(ticket.key)).data?.size).toBe(5);
    expect(fixture.logger.errors).toEqual([
      { message: 'cloudrop: onComplete failed for route "failing".', error: failure },
    ]);
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- Checking only size or only type: each mismatch row kills the other half of the `||`.
- Deleting an object whose `cloudrop-id` is not this upload's.
- Completing with ETags from the client, or skipping the ListParts check (R2 accepts wrong-sized parts until
  completion, spike Q4): the wrong-size row would complete or fail with `InvalidPart`.
- A retried completion after R2 forgot the upload answering `NO_SUCH_UPLOAD` instead of checking the object (§5.4).
- Completion without `If-None-Match: *` overwriting an object that appeared at the key.
- `onComplete` failure marked non-retryable, or the verified object deleted because of it.
- `onComplete` receiving the live `Date` while its type says string (the received context is compared exactly).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/complete.ts`:

```ts
// POST /complete (spec §5.4, §7.6): verify what the bucket holds against the token, finish a multipart upload with
// the server's own part list, run afterVerify and onComplete. The client's word is never taken for anything.

import { CloudropError, fail, ok, type Result } from "../errors.ts";
import type { CompleteResponse, UploadedFile } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import type { ObjectInfo } from "../storage/driver.ts";
import { encodeKeyPath } from "../storage/sigv4.ts";
import type { EndpointInput, ServerConfig } from "./config.ts";
import { storageFailure } from "./http.ts";
import { runAfterVerify, runOnAbort } from "./plugins.ts";
import { partCount, partSizeOf } from "./presign.ts";
import { isMultipart, type MultipartToken, type UploadTokenPayload } from "./token.ts";
import { abortContext, readTokenRequest } from "./token-request.ts";

const DISCARDED = "The uploaded file does not match what was declared, so it was discarded. Upload it again.";

async function discard(config: ServerConfig, token: UploadTokenPayload, error: CloudropError): Promise<Result<never>> {
  const removed = await config.storage.delete(token.key);
  if (removed.error) config.logger.error(`cloudrop: could not delete the rejected object ${token.key}.`, removed.error);
  await runOnAbort(config.plugins, [abortContext(token, "rejected")], config.logger);
  return fail(error);
}

/** Single PUT: the object must exist with this upload's id, size and type (spec §7.6). */
async function verifyObject(
  config: ServerConfig,
  token: UploadTokenPayload,
  object: ObjectInfo | null,
): Promise<Result<void>> {
  if (object === null) {
    return fail(
      new CloudropError("NOT_UPLOADED", "The file has not been uploaded yet. Upload it, then complete the upload."),
    );
  }
  // Another writer's object: never delete it.
  if (object.metadata["cloudrop-id"] !== token.fileId) {
    return fail(
      new CloudropError("VERIFICATION_FAILED", "Another file is stored under this upload's key. Start a new upload."),
    );
  }
  if (object.size !== token.size || object.type !== token.type) {
    return discard(config, token, new CloudropError("VERIFICATION_FAILED", DISCARDED));
  }
  return ok(undefined);
}

/** A multipart upload that no longer exists counts as complete only when HEAD shows its own object (spec §5.4). */
async function completedBefore(config: ServerConfig, token: MultipartToken): Promise<Result<void>> {
  const head = await config.storage.head(token.key);
  if (head.error) return fail(storageFailure(head.error, config.logger));
  if (head.data?.metadata["cloudrop-id"] === token.fileId && head.data.size === token.size) return ok(undefined);
  return fail(new CloudropError("NO_SUCH_UPLOAD", "This upload was aborted or has expired. Start it again."));
}

/** Multipart: ListParts against the plan, then completion with the server's part list and ETags (spec §7.6, §12). */
async function completeParts(config: ServerConfig, token: MultipartToken): Promise<Result<void>> {
  const { storage, logger } = config;
  const listed = await storage.listParts(token.key, token.uploadId);
  if (listed.error) {
    return listed.error.code === "NO_SUCH_UPLOAD"
      ? completedBefore(config, token)
      : fail(storageFailure(listed.error, logger));
  }
  const byNumber = new Map(listed.data.map((part) => [part.partNumber, part]));
  const numbers = Array.from({ length: partCount(token) }, (_, index) => index + 1);
  // A wrong-sized part is re-sent like a missing one: R2 accepts it at UploadPart and rejects it only at completion.
  const missing = numbers.filter((partNumber) => byNumber.get(partNumber)?.size !== partSizeOf(token, partNumber));
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    return fail(
      new CloudropError(
        "PARTS_MISSING",
        `${missing.length} of ${numbers.length} parts are missing or have the wrong size. Upload them again, then complete the upload.`,
        { parts: missing },
      ),
    );
  }
  const parts = numbers.map((partNumber) => ({ partNumber, etag: byNumber.get(partNumber)?.etag ?? "" }));
  // Keys are unique, so a 412 here is a collision with an object some other writer created.
  const completed = await storage.completeMultipart(token.key, token.uploadId, parts, { ifNoneMatch: true });
  if (!completed.error) return ok(undefined);
  if (completed.error.code === "NO_SUCH_UPLOAD") return completedBefore(config, token);
  if (completed.error.code === "ALREADY_EXISTS") {
    await storage.abortMultipart(token.key, token.uploadId);
    await runOnAbort(config.plugins, [abortContext(token, "rejected")], logger);
    return fail(new CloudropError("ALREADY_EXISTS", "Another file already has this upload's key. Start a new upload."));
  }
  return fail(storageFailure(completed.error, logger));
}

export async function handleComplete({ request, body, config }: EndpointInput): Promise<Result<CompleteResponse>> {
  const verified = await readTokenRequest(body, config);
  if (verified.error) return verified;
  const { token, route } = verified.data;
  const { storage, logger } = config;
  let stored: Result<void>;
  if (isMultipart(token)) {
    stored = await completeParts(config, token);
  } else {
    const head = await storage.head(token.key);
    stored = head.error ? fail(storageFailure(head.error, logger)) : await verifyObject(config, token, head.data);
  }
  if (stored.error) return stored;
  const file: UploadedFile = {
    id: token.fileId,
    key: token.key,
    name: token.name,
    size: token.size,
    type: token.type,
    url: route.visibility === "public" ? `${storage.publicUrl}/${encodeKeyPath(token.key)}` : null,
  };
  const context = { request, route: route.name, file, metadata: token.metadata, auth: token.owner, storage };
  const refused = await runAfterVerify(config.plugins, context);
  if (refused) return discard(config, token, refused);
  const { onComplete } = route.definition;
  if (!onComplete) return ok({ file, data: null });
  try {
    // /upload checked that authorize returned an object; the token carries its JSON.
    const data = await onComplete({ ...(token.owner as object), request, file, metadata: token.metadata });
    return ok({ file, data: data === undefined ? null : data });
  } catch (error) {
    logger.error(`cloudrop: onComplete failed for route "${route.name}".`, error);
    return fail(
      new CloudropError(
        "SERVER_ERROR",
        "The file was uploaded, but the server could not finish processing it. Try again in a moment.",
        { retryable: true },
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### Task 12: Type inference for the client

Spec §11.5: `typeof cloudrop` gives the client its route names, metadata input types and result types. The types exist
since Tasks 1, 7 and 8; this task proves them.

**Files:**
- Test: `packages/cloudrop/src/server/types.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `route`, `allowAnyone`, `unauthorized`, `createCloudrop`, `RouteName`, `RouteMetadata`, `RouteResult`,
  `StandardSchema`.
- Produces: no code; the type contract M3's `useUpload<typeof cloudrop>` relies on.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the type tests**

`bun run typecheck` compiles this file; the runtime tests only keep Vitest from reporting an empty file. The `getUser`
and schema values are real, so the module loads under Vitest.

Create `packages/cloudrop/src/server/types.test.ts`:

```ts
// Type inference tests (spec §11.5). `bun run typecheck` compiles this file: every expectTypeOf and every
// expect-error directive is an assertion. The runtime test only keeps Vitest from reporting an empty file.

import { describe, expectTypeOf, it } from "vitest";
import type { RouteMetadata, RouteName, RouteResult } from "../protocol/types.ts";
import { r2 } from "../storage/presets.ts";
import { createCloudrop } from "./cloudrop.ts";
import { allowAnyone, route, unauthorized } from "./route.ts";
import type { StandardSchema } from "./schema.ts";

interface User {
  id: string;
  joined: Date;
}
const getUser = async (request: Request): Promise<User | null> =>
  request.headers.has("cookie") ? { id: "42", joined: new Date(0) } : null;
const albumSchema: StandardSchema<{ albumId: string }, { albumId: string; takenAt: Date }> = {
  "~standard": {
    version: 1,
    vendor: "test",
    validate: (value) => ({
      value: { albumId: String((value as { albumId: unknown }).albumId), takenAt: new Date(0) },
    }),
  },
};

const avatar = route({
  accept: "image/*",
  maxSize: "5MB",
  metadata: albumSchema,
  authorize: async ({ request, metadata }) => {
    expectTypeOf(metadata).toEqualTypeOf<{ albumId: string; takenAt: Date }>();
    const user = await getUser(request);
    return user ? { user } : unauthorized();
  },
  prefix: ({ user, metadata, file }) => {
    expectTypeOf(user).toEqualTypeOf<User>();
    expectTypeOf(file.ext).toEqualTypeOf<string>();
    return `users/${user.id}/${metadata.albumId}`;
  },
  onComplete: ({ user, metadata, file }) => {
    // onComplete reads the token's JSON copy: Dates are strings there, and the types say so.
    expectTypeOf(user).toEqualTypeOf<{ id: string; joined: string }>();
    expectTypeOf(metadata).toEqualTypeOf<{ albumId: string; takenAt: string }>();
    return { url: file.url, at: new Date() };
  },
});

const notes = route({ accept: "text/plain", maxSize: 1024, authorize: allowAnyone });

const cloudrop = createCloudrop({
  storage: r2({
    bucket: "uploads",
    accountId: "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    accessKeyId: "k",
    secretAccessKey: "s",
  }),
  secret: "test-secret-0123456789abcdef0123456789",
  routes: { avatar, notes },
});

describe("type inference for the client", () => {
  it("is checked by the compiler", () => {
    expectTypeOf<RouteName<typeof cloudrop>>().toEqualTypeOf<"avatar" | "notes">();
    expectTypeOf<RouteMetadata<typeof cloudrop, "avatar">>().toEqualTypeOf<{ albumId: string }>();
    expectTypeOf<RouteMetadata<typeof cloudrop, "notes">>().toEqualTypeOf<undefined>();
    expectTypeOf<RouteResult<typeof cloudrop, "avatar">>().toEqualTypeOf<{ url: string | null; at: string }>();
    expectTypeOf<RouteResult<typeof cloudrop, "notes">>().toEqualTypeOf<null>();
    // @ts-expect-error: there is no route named "banner".
    expectTypeOf<RouteResult<typeof cloudrop, "banner">>();
  });

  it("requires authorize", () => {
    // @ts-expect-error: a route without authorize does not type-check; public routes say authorize: allowAnyone.
    route({ accept: "*/*", maxSize: 1 });
  });

  it("infers the authorize result whatever order the options are written in", () => {
    route({
      accept: "*/*",
      maxSize: 1,
      onComplete: ({ teamId }) => expectTypeOf(teamId).toEqualTypeOf<string>(),
      authorize: () => ({ teamId: "t1" }),
    });
  });
});
```

Bugs these tests catch, beyond the obvious ones:

- `onComplete` typed with the live `authorize` result instead of its JSON form (`joined: Date` instead of `string`).
- `RouteMetadata` giving the schema's output instead of its input (the client sends input).
- `RouteResult` not awaited or not `Jsonified` (`at: Date` instead of `string`).
- A route without `authorize` type-checking.
- Inference that depends on `authorize` being written before `onComplete`.

(§10).

**Files:**

- Test: `packages/cloudrop/src/server/api.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StorageDriver` (with Task 6's methods), `keyProblem`, `prefixProblem`, `encodeKeyPath`,
  `contentDisposition`, `nowSeconds`, `invalidInput`, `fromEnv`.
- Produces (`bucket-check.ts`): `interface BucketCheck { status: "ok" | "problem" | "unknown"; message: string }`,
  `interface BucketReport { cors; lifecycle; publicAccess: BucketCheck }`, `checkBucket(config: ServerConfig, origins:
  readonly string[]): Promise<Result<BucketReport>>`.
- Produces (`api.ts`): `interface FileApi { getUrl; head; put; delete; deleteMany; copy; deletePrefix; checkBucket }`
  with the signatures in the code below; `createFileApi(config: ServerConfig): FileApi`.
- `Cloudrop` gains `readonly api: FileApi`; the handler runs `checkBucket` once in development.
